01 · Student A
Project setup and reproducible release selection

Explain the major-version anchors, the calendar-gap rule, the 13.0.1 exception and how a selected artifact is tied to a tag and commit.

Commit message: import: add project setup and reproducible Guava release selection (AI-assisted preparation)

Checks during import:

python scripts/select_versions.py --check
PYTHONPATH=src python -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_selection.py' -v

The complete file inventory and the commands for this packet are in code/01-a-setup.md. Every source block below contains the whole file.

SHA-256: 7e3f2beb52a7a85b5aab78a684ed2f75a3e3caf2bdd45522b765ebe6705dac4b

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Select 30 Guava releases by major coverage and recorded calendar gaps.

Default/--check validates without writing; --write preserves matching artifact
pins. Selection reads only the frozen release catalog and declared parameters.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import statistics

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
COUNT, FIRST_MAJOR, LAST_MAJOR = 30, 10, 33
CUTOFF = "2026-10-17"
MINIMUM_GAP_DAYS, MAX_OPTIONAL_PER_MAJOR = 60, 2
OLDEST_VERSION, LATEST_VERSION = "10.0", "33.7.2"
METHOD = "major_anchors_calendar_greedy_v3"
SOURCE_PREFIXES = ["com/google/common/", "com/google/thirdparty/publicsuffix/"]
SOURCE_ROOTS = ["guava/src/", "src/", "guava/src/main/java/", "src/main/java/"]
DATE_BASIS = ("Whole calendar-day differences: documented dates for date-only records, "
              "UTC calendar dates for timestamped records; no fractional-day weighting.")
PARAMETERS = {
    "requested_count": COUNT, "first_major": FIRST_MAJOR, "last_major": LAST_MAJOR,
    "oldest_endpoint_version": OLDEST_VERSION, "latest_endpoint_version": LATEST_VERSION,
    "representative_rule": "first_eligible_stable_release_per_family", "optional_count": 5,
    "minimum_calendar_gap_days": MINIMUM_GAP_DAYS,
    "max_optional_families_per_major": MAX_OPTIONAL_PER_MAJOR,
    "objective": "greedy_reduction_in_sum_squared_calendar_gaps",
    "tie_break": ["earlier_utc_calendar_date", "numeric_version"],
    "latest_endpoint_counts_as_optional": False,
}
POLICY = (
    "From the frozen 2026-10-17 catalog retain the same 70 eligible stable main-project "
    "releases in 45 MAJOR.MINOR families and the existing three documented mainline "
    "exclusions. Use the first eligible stable release in each family. Require every "
    "MAJOR.0 family from 10 through 33 (24 anchors, including 13.0.1 because ambiguous "
    "13.0 is excluded), preserving the fixed oldest 10.0 and latest 33.7.2 endpoints. "
    "The latest endpoint is the sole exception to the first-release representative "
    "rule and does not count as an optional addition. Fill five remaining slots "
    "greedily: splitting a calendar gap into L and R days reduces the sum of squared "
    "gaps by 2*L*R. Choose the greatest reduction, then the earlier UTC calendar date, "
    "then numeric version. Keep every selected gap at least 60 calendar days and "
    "permit at most two optional minor-family additions in any major. Each selected "
    "family appears once. This balances major-history coverage and calendar spacing; "
    "it does not require an omitted numeric tag between every pair or claim a globally "
    "optimal calendar design. Use plain Maven artifacts before 23.1 and JRE artifacts "
    "thereafter. Selection reads no graphs, clusters, quality values or structural-change outcomes."
)


def version_key(version):
    if not re.fullmatch(r"\d+\.\d+(?:\.\d+)?", version):
        raise ValueError(f"Not a numeric stable version: {version}")
    parts = tuple(int(p) for p in version.split("."))
    return parts + (0,) * (3 - len(parts))


def family(version):
    version_key(version)
    return ".".join(version.split(".")[:2])


def release_day(row):
    value = row["release_date"]
    if re.fullmatch(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", value):
        return date.fromisoformat(value)
    instant = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if instant.tzinfo is None:
        raise ValueError("Timestamped release dates must specify their timezone.")
    return instant.astimezone(timezone.utc).date()


def row_key(row):
    return release_day(row), version_key(row["version"])


def greedy_fill_gaps(representatives, mandatory, target_count, minimum_gap_days,
                     max_optional_per_major):
    """Pure selector. Score 2LR=(L+R)^2-L^2-R^2; cap counts only new additions."""
    if minimum_gap_days < 1 or max_optional_per_major < 0:
        raise ValueError("Gap must be positive and optional-major limit nonnegative.")
    if target_count < len(mandatory):
        raise ValueError("Requested count cannot discard mandatory anchors.")
    candidates, selected = sorted(representatives, key=row_key), sorted(mandatory, key=row_key)
    for rows in (candidates, selected):
        if len({family(r["version"]) for r in rows}) != len(rows):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate representative or mandatory family.")
    if len(selected) < 2 or any(r not in candidates for r in selected):
        raise ValueError("Mandatory endpoints must be candidate representatives.")
    if any((release_day(b) - release_day(a)).days < minimum_gap_days
           for a, b in zip(selected, selected[1:])):
        raise ValueError("Mandatory anchors cannot satisfy the requested calendar gap.")
    counts, steps = Counter(), []
    while len(selected) < target_count:
        selected_families = {family(r["version"]) for r in selected}
        options = []
        for candidate in candidates:
            major = version_key(candidate["version"])[0]
            if family(candidate["version"]) in selected_families or counts[major] >= max_optional_per_major:
                continue
            day = release_day(candidate)
            pos = next((i for i, r in enumerate(selected) if release_day(r) > day), len(selected))
            if pos == 0 or pos == len(selected):
                continue
            left, right = selected[pos - 1:pos + 1]
            left_days, right_days = (day - release_day(left)).days, (release_day(right) - day).days
            if min(left_days, right_days) < minimum_gap_days:
                continue
            key = (-2 * left_days * right_days, day, version_key(candidate["version"]))
            options.append((key, candidate, left, right, left_days, right_days))
        if not options:
            raise ValueError("Too few families satisfy the calendar gap and optional-major cap.")
        key, candidate, left, right, left_days, right_days = min(options, key=lambda x: x[0])
        major = version_key(candidate["version"])[0]
        counts[major] += 1
        steps.append({
            "step": len(steps) + 1, "version": candidate["version"],
            "family": family(candidate["version"]), "calendar_date": release_day(candidate).isoformat(),
            "between_versions": [left["version"], right["version"]],
            "original_gap_days": left_days + right_days,
            "left_gap_days": left_days, "right_gap_days": right_days,
            "squared_gap_reduction_days2": -key[0],
            "optional_count_for_major_after_selection": counts[major],
            "feasible_candidate_count": len(options),
        })
        selected.append(candidate)
        selected.sort(key=row_key)
    return deepcopy(selected), steps


def selection_details(inventory):
    stable = inventory["stable_tags_in_date_order"]
    if len({r["version"] for r in stable}) != len(stable):
        raise ValueError("Captured catalog contains duplicate versions.")
    exclusions = {r["version"] for r in inventory["additional_exclusions"]}
    eligible = sorted((r for r in stable if FIRST_MAJOR <= version_key(r["version"])[0] <= LAST_MAJOR
                       and r["version"] not in exclusions
                       and release_day(r) <= date.fromisoformat(CUTOFF)), key=row_key)
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for row in eligible:
        groups[family(row["version"])].append(row)
    names = sorted(groups, key=lambda name: row_key(groups[name][0]))
    if len(eligible) != 70 or len(names) != 45:
        raise ValueError("Frozen catalog must produce 70 eligible releases and 45 families.")
    representatives = {name: rows[0] for name, rows in groups.items()}
    latest = next((r for r in eligible if r["version"] == LATEST_VERSION), None)
    if latest is None or eligible[-1]["version"] != LATEST_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("The frozen latest endpoint is missing or inconsistent.")
    representatives[family(LATEST_VERSION)] = latest
    anchor_names = [f"{major}.0" for major in range(FIRST_MAJOR, LAST_MAJOR + 1)]
    if any(name not in representatives for name in anchor_names):
        raise ValueError("Every major from 10 through 33 must have a MAJOR.0 anchor.")
    mandatory = [representatives[name] for name in anchor_names] + [latest]
    chosen, steps = greedy_fill_gaps(list(representatives.values()), mandatory, COUNT,
                                     MINIMUM_GAP_DAYS, MAX_OPTIONAL_PER_MAJOR)
    if chosen[0]["version"] != OLDEST_VERSION or chosen[-1]["version"] != LATEST_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("Unexpected frozen-history endpoints.")
    selected_names = {family(r["version"]) for r in chosen}
    audit = []
    for rank, name in enumerate(names):
        audit.append({
            "family_rank_zero_based": rank, "family": name,
            "eligible_versions": [r["version"] for r in groups[name]],
            "first_release_date": groups[name][0]["release_date"],
            "representative": representatives[name]["version"],
            "representative_calendar_date": release_day(representatives[name]).isoformat(),
            "representative_rule": ("latest_history_endpoint" if name == family(LATEST_VERSION)
                                    else "first_eligible_stable_release"),
            "selected": name in selected_names,
            "selection_role": ("major_anchor" if name in anchor_names else
                               "latest_endpoint" if name == family(LATEST_VERSION) else
                               "gap_filling_minor" if name in selected_names else None),
        })
    ranks = [names.index(family(r["version"])) for r in chosen]
    return chosen, audit, ranks, steps, sorted(mandatory, key=row_key)


def selected_rows(inventory):
    """Preserve the pure three-result interface used by acquisition callers."""
    chosen, audit, ranks, _, _ = selection_details(inventory)
    return chosen, audit, ranks


def calendar_gaps(rows):
    return [{"from_version": a["version"], "to_version": b["version"],
             "from_date": release_day(a).isoformat(), "to_date": release_day(b).isoformat(),
             "gap_days": (release_day(b) - release_day(a)).days}
            for a, b in zip(rows, rows[1:])]


def gap_statistics(rows):
    gaps = [g["gap_days"] for g in calendar_gaps(rows)]
    return {"count": len(gaps), "minimum_days": min(gaps), "median_days": statistics.median(gaps),
            "maximum_days": max(gaps), "mean_days": statistics.mean(gaps),
            "total_span_days": sum(gaps),
            "below_declared_minimum_count": sum(g < MINIMUM_GAP_DAYS for g in gaps)}


def validate_manifest(manifest, inventory):
    chosen, audit, ranks, _, _ = selection_details(inventory)
    rows = manifest["versions"]
    if manifest.get("schema_version") != 4 or manifest.get("study_revision") != 3:
        raise ValueError("Selection requires manifest schema 4 / study revision 3.")
    if (manifest.get("selection_method") != METHOD or manifest.get("selection_parameters") != PARAMETERS
            or manifest.get("selection_policy") != POLICY):
        raise ValueError("Declared selection policy or parameters differ from the frozen rule.")
    if len(rows) != COUNT or len({family(r["version"]) for r in rows}) != COUNT:
        raise ValueError("Exactly 30 releases from 30 distinct families are required.")
    if [r["version"] for r in rows] != [r["version"] for r in chosen]:
        raise ValueError("Selected releases differ from the major-anchor calendar rule.")
    stable = sorted(inventory["stable_tags_in_date_order"], key=row_key)
    positions = {r["version"]: i for i, r in enumerate(stable)}
    for i, (row, captured) in enumerate(zip(rows, chosen)):
        for key in ("tag", "commit_sha", "release_date", "date_basis"):
            if row[key] != captured[key]:
                raise ValueError(f"Captured {key} differs for {row['version']}")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-f0-9]{40}", row["commit_sha"]):
            raise ValueError("Missing immutable commit SHA.")
        if row["family"] != family(row["version"]) or row["family_rank_zero_based"] != ranks[i]:
            raise ValueError(f"Incorrect family metadata for {row['version']}")
        for key in ("representative_rule", "selection_role"):
            if row.get(key) != audit[ranks[i]][key]:
                raise ValueError(f"Incorrect {key} for {row['version']}")
        skipped = [] if i == 0 else [r["version"] for r in stable[
            positions[rows[i - 1]["version"]] + 1:positions[row["version"]]]]
        omitted = [] if i == 0 else [r["family"] for r in audit[ranks[i - 1] + 1:ranks[i]]]
        if row["skipped_stable_since_previous"] != skipped or row["skipped_families_since_previous"] != omitted:
            raise ValueError(f"Incorrect intervening releases or families for {row['version']}")
        if row.get("source_prefixes") != SOURCE_PREFIXES:
            raise ValueError("Guava ownership scope must remain unchanged.")
        gap = None if i == 0 else (release_day(row) - release_day(rows[i - 1])).days
        if row.get("calendar_gap_days_since_previous") != gap:
            raise ValueError(f"Incorrect calendar gap for {row['version']}")
    expected = {"major_minor_families": [family(r["version"]) for r in rows],
                "selected_family_ranks_zero_based": ranks, "eligible_major_minor_families": len(audit),
                "selected_major_numbers": list(range(FIRST_MAJOR, LAST_MAJOR + 1)),
                "calendar_gap_statistics": gap_statistics(chosen)}
    for key, value in expected.items():
        if manifest.get(key) != value:
            raise ValueError(f"Top-level {key} differs from the audited selection.")


def build_manifest(previous, inventory, catalog_sha256):
    chosen, audit, ranks, steps, mandatory = selection_details(inventory)
    previous_rows = {r["version"]: r for r in previous["versions"]}
    stable = sorted(inventory["stable_tags_in_date_order"], key=row_key)
    positions = {r["version"]: i for i, r in enumerate(stable)}
    rows = []
    for i, (captured, rank) in enumerate(zip(chosen, ranks)):
        version, row = captured["version"], deepcopy(captured)
        artifact_version = version + ("-jre" if version_key(version) >= (23, 1, 0) else "")
        row.update({
            "index": i + 1, "family": family(version), "family_rank_zero_based": rank,
            "representative_rule": audit[rank]["representative_rule"],
            "selection_role": audit[rank]["selection_role"],
            "calendar_gap_days_since_previous": None if i == 0 else
                (release_day(row) - release_day(rows[-1])).days,
            "artifact_version": artifact_version, "group_id": "com.google.guava", "artifact_id": "guava",
            "source_prefix": SOURCE_PREFIXES[0], "source_prefixes": SOURCE_PREFIXES,
            "source_roots": SOURCE_ROOTS,
            "skipped_stable_since_previous": [] if i == 0 else [r["version"] for r in stable[
                positions[rows[-1]["version"]] + 1:positions[version]]],
            "skipped_families_since_previous": [] if i == 0 else [r["family"] for r in audit[ranks[i - 1] + 1:rank]],
        })
        for kind, suffix in (("jar", ".jar"), ("sources", "-sources.jar"), ("pom", ".pom")):
            filename = f"guava-{artifact_version}{suffix}"
            row[f"{kind}_url"] = f"https://repo.maven.apache.org/maven2/com/google/guava/guava/{artifact_version}/{filename}"
            row[f"{kind}_path"] = f"data/raw/{version}/{filename}"
            old = previous_rows.get(version, {})
            if old.get(f"{kind}_url") == row[f"{kind}_url"]:
                for checksum in ("sha256", "maven_sha1"):
                    key = f"{kind}_{checksum}"
                    if old.get(key):
                        row[key] = old[key]
        rows.append(row)
    manifest = deepcopy(previous)
    manifest.update({
        "schema_version": 4, "study_revision": 3, "selection_date_utc": CUTOFF,
        "selection_method": METHOD, "selection_parameters": deepcopy(PARAMETERS),
        "selection_policy": POLICY, "selection_catalog_sha256": catalog_sha256,
        "eligible_major_minor_families": len(audit), "selected_count": COUNT,
        "selected_family_ranks_zero_based": ranks,
        "major_minor_families": [family(r["version"]) for r in rows],
        "selected_major_numbers": list(range(FIRST_MAJOR, LAST_MAJOR + 1)),
        "calendar_gap_statistics": gap_statistics(rows), "calendar_date_basis": DATE_BASIS,
        "versions": rows,
    })
    validate_manifest(manifest, inventory)
    anchor_gaps = calendar_gaps(mandatory)
    minimum_anchor_gap = min(g["gap_days"] for g in anchor_gaps)
    evidence = {
        "schema_version": 2, "study_revision": 3, "frozen_cutoff_utc": CUTOFF,
        "catalog_path": "data/provenance/tag_inventory.json", "catalog_sha256": catalog_sha256,
        "selection_method": METHOD, "selection_parameters": deepcopy(PARAMETERS), "rule": POLICY,
        "eligible_releases": 70, "eligible_families": len(audit), "selected_count": COUNT,
        "selected_family_ranks_zero_based": ranks, "selected_versions": [r["version"] for r in rows],
        "selected_unique_families": COUNT, "unselected_families": len(audit) - COUNT,
        "selected_major_numbers": list(range(FIRST_MAJOR, LAST_MAJOR + 1)),
        "mandatory_anchors": [{
            "version": r["version"], "family": family(r["version"]), "release_date": r["release_date"],
            "calendar_date": release_day(r).isoformat(),
            "selection_role": "latest_endpoint" if r["version"] == LATEST_VERSION else "major_anchor",
            "rationale": ("Fixed latest stable endpoint; not charged to the optional-major budget."
                          if r["version"] == LATEST_VERSION else
                          "Cover this major's MAJOR.0 milestone with its first eligible stable release."),
        } for r in mandatory],
        "optional_selection_steps": steps, "calendar_date_basis": DATE_BASIS,
        "calendar_gaps": calendar_gaps(rows), "calendar_gap_statistics": gap_statistics(rows),
        "spacing_feasibility": {
            "mandatory_anchor_minimum_gap_days": minimum_anchor_gap,
            "limiting_anchor_pairs": [g for g in anchor_gaps if g["gap_days"] == minimum_anchor_gap],
            "ninety_day_floor_feasible_with_fixed_anchors": minimum_anchor_gap >= 90,
            "note": "A floor above the smallest fixed-anchor gap requires changing an anchor. The greedy objective is not claimed globally optimal.",
        },
        "family_catalog": audit,
        "selection_inputs_include": ["captured stable tag identities", "recorded release dates", "numeric version families", "declared parameters"],
        "selection_inputs_exclude": ["dependency graphs", "clustering outcomes", "quality metrics", "structural identity", "source-code changes"],
    }
    return manifest, evidence


def write_json(path, data):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\n")
    temporary.replace(path)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=ROOT / "config/versions.json")
    parser.add_argument("--catalog", type=Path, default=ROOT / "data/provenance/tag_inventory.json")
    action = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    action.add_argument("--write", action="store_true")
    action.add_argument("--check", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    catalog_bytes = args.catalog.read_bytes()
    catalog_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(catalog_bytes).hexdigest()
    inventory, existing = json.loads(catalog_bytes), json.loads(args.manifest.read_text())
    if args.write:
        revised, evidence = build_manifest(existing, inventory, catalog_sha256)
        write_json(args.manifest, revised)
        write_json(ROOT / "data/provenance/family_selection.json", evidence)
    else:
        validate_manifest(existing, inventory)
        if existing.get("selection_catalog_sha256") != catalog_sha256:
            raise ValueError("Catalog bytes do not match the recorded SHA-256.")
        revised = existing
    print(json.dumps({"selected_count": len(revised["versions"]),
                      "unique_families": len(revised["major_minor_families"]),
                      "major_numbers": revised["selected_major_numbers"],
                      "calendar_gap_statistics": revised["calendar_gap_statistics"],
                      "versions": [r["version"] for r in revised["versions"]],
                      "mode": "write" if args.write else "check"}, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Reproducible Guava architecture evolution study for EPL484."""

__version__ = "1.0.0"

"""Validated, scoped graph inputs and deterministic audit-file helpers."""
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
from dataclasses import dataclass
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path


def read_csv(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def write_csv(path: Path, rows: list[dict], fieldnames: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    columns = fieldnames or (list(rows[0]) if rows else [])
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=columns, lineterminator="\n", extrasaction="raise")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def write_json(path: Path, value) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        return hashlib.file_digest(handle, "sha256").hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Graph:
    nodes: tuple[str, ...]
    edges: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]

    def __post_init__(self):
        if len(self.nodes) != len(set(self.nodes)):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate graph nodes")
        if len(self.edges) != len(set(self.edges)):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate graph edges: expected binary graph")
        nodes = set(self.nodes)
        if any(a not in nodes or b not in nodes or a == b for a, b in self.edges):
            raise ValueError("Out-of-scope endpoint or self loop")

    @property
    def isolates(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        active = {v for edge in self.edges for v in edge}
        return tuple(n for n in self.nodes if n not in active)

    def without(self, removed) -> "Graph":
        removed = set(removed)
        if not removed <= set(self.nodes):
            raise ValueError("Attempt to remove nonexistent graph nodes")
        return Graph(tuple(n for n in self.nodes if n not in removed),
                     tuple((a, b) for a, b in self.edges if a not in removed and b not in removed))

    def rsf(self, path: Path) -> None:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text("".join(f"call {a} {b}\n" for a, b in self.edges))


def read_graph(directory: Path) -> Graph:
    nodes = tuple(row["class"] for row in read_csv(directory / "classes.csv"))
    edges = tuple((row["source"], row["target"]) for row in read_csv(directory / "dependencies.csv"))
    return Graph(nodes, edges)

EXPECTED = (
    "10.0,11.0,12.0,13.0.1,14.0,15.0,16.0,17.0,18.0,19.0,20.0,21.0,22.0,23.0,"
    "24.0,25.0,26.0,27.0,27.1,28.0,28.2,29.0,30.0,31.0,31.1,32.0.0,33.0.0,"

def dated(version, day):
    return {"version": version, "release_date": (date(2020, 1, 1) + timedelta(days=day)).isoformat()}


class CalendarSelectorTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_gap_boundary_is_inclusive_and_infeasible_gap_fails(self):
        left, middle, right = dated("10.0", 0), dated("11.0", 60), dated("12.0", 120)
        chosen, steps = selector.greedy_fill_gaps([right, middle, left], [left, right], 3, 60, 2)
        self.assertEqual([r["version"] for r in chosen], ["10.0", "11.0", "12.0"])
        self.assertEqual(steps[0]["squared_gap_reduction_days2"], 120**2 - 60**2 - 60**2)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.greedy_fill_gaps([left, middle, right], [left, right], 3, 61, 2)

    def test_equal_gain_uses_earlier_calendar_date_then_numeric_version(self):
        left, right = dated("10.0", 0), dated("20.0", 180)
        early, late = dated("15.0", 60), dated("11.0", 120)
        _, steps = selector.greedy_fill_gaps([right, late, early, left], [left, right], 3, 60, 2)
        self.assertEqual(steps[0]["version"], "15.0")
        two, ten = dated("11.2", 90), dated("11.10", 90)
        for candidates in ([left, ten, two, right], [right, two, ten, left]):
            _, steps = selector.greedy_fill_gaps(candidates, [left, right], 3, 60, 2)
            self.assertEqual(steps[0]["version"], "11.2")

    def test_optional_cap_does_not_charge_the_mandatory_endpoint(self):
        left, right = dated("10.0", 0), dated("15.7", 360)
        a, b = dated("15.4", 120), dated("15.5", 240)
        candidates = [left, a, b, right]
        chosen, steps = selector.greedy_fill_gaps(candidates, [left, right], 4, 60, 2)
        self.assertEqual(len(chosen), 4)
        self.assertEqual([s["optional_count_for_major_after_selection"] for s in steps], [1, 2])
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.greedy_fill_gaps(candidates, [left, right], 4, 60, 1)

    def test_duplicate_family_cannot_be_used_to_reach_requested_count(self):
        left, right = dated("10.0", 0), dated("20.0", 360)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.greedy_fill_gaps([left, dated("11.0", 120), dated("11.0.1", 240), right],
                                      [left, right], 4, 60, 2)

    def test_dates_are_whole_utc_calendar_days_and_naive_timestamps_fail(self):
        self.assertEqual(selector.release_day({"release_date": "2020-01-02T00:30:00+02:00"}),
                         date(2020, 1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(selector.release_day({"release_date": "2020-01-01T23:59:59Z"}),
                         selector.release_day({"release_date": "2020-01-01"}))
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.release_day({"release_date": "2020-01-01T12:00:00"})


class FrozenSelectionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        raw = (ROOT / "data/provenance/tag_inventory.json").read_bytes()
        cls.catalog, cls.catalog_hash = json.loads(raw), hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        cls.manifest, cls.evidence = selector.build_manifest({"versions": []}, cls.catalog, cls.catalog_hash)

    def test_approved_sample_covers_every_major_and_fixes_both_endpoints(self):
        rows = self.manifest["versions"]
        self.assertEqual([r["version"] for r in rows], EXPECTED)
        self.assertEqual(len({r["family"] for r in rows}), 30)
        self.assertEqual({int(r["version"].split(".")[0]) for r in rows}, set(range(10, 34)))
        self.assertTrue({f"{major}.0" for major in range(10, 34)} <= {r["family"] for r in rows})
        self.assertEqual(sum(r["selection_role"] == "major_anchor" for r in rows), 24)
        self.assertEqual(sum(r["selection_role"] == "latest_endpoint" for r in rows), 1)
        self.assertEqual(sum(r["selection_role"] == "gap_filling_minor" for r in rows), 5)
        self.assertEqual((self.manifest["schema_version"], self.manifest["study_revision"]), (4, 3))

    def test_actual_dates_give_the_declared_spacing_and_ninety_days_is_infeasible(self):
        dates = [date.fromisoformat(r["release_date"][:10]) for r in self.manifest["versions"]]
        gaps = [(b - a).days for a, b in zip(dates, dates[1:])]
        self.assertEqual((min(gaps), sorted(gaps)[14], max(gaps)), (74, 141, 471))
        self.assertTrue(all(g >= 60 for g in gaps))
        _, audit, _, _, mandatory = selector.selection_details(self.catalog)
        representatives = [{"version": r["representative"], "release_date": r["representative_calendar_date"]}
                           for r in audit]
        mandatory = [{"version": r["version"], "release_date": r["release_date"][:10]} for r in mandatory]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Mandatory anchors"):
            selector.greedy_fill_gaps(representatives, mandatory, 30, 90, 2)

    def test_catalog_order_and_outcome_fields_do_not_change_the_selection(self):
        changed = deepcopy(self.catalog)
        changed["stable_tags_in_date_order"].reverse()
        for i, row in enumerate(changed["stable_tags_in_date_order"]):
            row["quality_score"] = i * -1000
            row["classes"] = 0
        rows, _, _ = selector.selected_rows(changed)
        self.assertEqual([r["version"] for r in rows], EXPECTED)
        self.assertEqual(selector.selection_details(changed)[3], self.evidence["optional_selection_steps"])

    def test_corrupt_catalog_and_future_endpoint_are_rejected(self):
        duplicate = deepcopy(self.catalog)
        duplicate["stable_tags_in_date_order"].append(deepcopy(duplicate["stable_tags_in_date_order"][0]))
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.selected_rows(duplicate)
        future = deepcopy(self.catalog)
        next(r for r in future["stable_tags_in_date_order"] if r["version"] == "33.7.2")["release_date"] = "2026-10-18"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.selected_rows(future)

    def test_manifest_rejects_changed_identity_dates_roles_and_gap_metadata(self):
        changes = [
            (0, "commit_sha", "0" * 40), (0, "release_date", "2011-09-29"),
            (1, "calendar_gap_days_since_previous", 82),
            (1, "skipped_stable_since_previous", ["33.7.2"]),
            (0, "selection_role", "gap_filling_minor"),
        ]
        for index, key, value in changes:
            with self.subTest(key=key):
                changed = deepcopy(self.manifest)
                changed["versions"][index][key] = value
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    selector.validate_manifest(changed, self.catalog)
        changed = deepcopy(self.manifest)
        changed["versions"][1] = deepcopy(changed["versions"][0])
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            selector.validate_manifest(changed, self.catalog)

    def test_only_matching_artifact_urls_preserve_existing_checksum_pins(self):
        prior = deepcopy(self.manifest)
        for kind in ("jar", "sources", "pom"):
            prior["versions"][0][kind + "_sha256"] = "a" * 64
            prior["versions"][0][kind + "_maven_sha1"] = "b" * 40
        prior["versions"][0]["pom_url"] += ".wrong"
        rebuilt, _ = selector.build_manifest(prior, self.catalog, self.catalog_hash)
        row = rebuilt["versions"][0]
        self.assertEqual(row["jar_sha256"], "a" * 64)
        self.assertEqual(row["sources_maven_sha1"], "b" * 40)
        self.assertNotIn("pom_sha256", row)
        self.assertNotIn("pom_maven_sha1", row)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Benchmark JNode-UE on a deterministic graph with analytically known scores."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import gc
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import resource
import statistics
import sys
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
from evolution.omnipresent import detect_omnipresent


def run_benchmark(sizes: list[int], degree: int = 20, repeats: int = 3) -> dict:
    if not sizes or degree < 1 or repeats < 1 or any(n <= degree for n in sizes):
        raise ValueError("sizes must exceed the positive degree; repeats must be positive")
    cases = []
    for count in sizes:
        classes = [f"C{i:05d}" for i in range(count)]
        owners = {f"{c}#run()V": c for c in classes}
        owners.update({f"{c}#accept()V": c for c in classes})
        edges = [
            (f"{classes[i]}#run()V", f"{classes[(i + j) % count]}#accept()V")
            for i in range(count) for j in range(1, degree + 1)
        ]
        expected_equality = 1 - 1 / degree
        expected_removed = count if degree >= 10 else 0
        durations = []
        for _ in range(repeats):
            gc.collect()
            start = time.perf_counter()
            result = detect_omnipresent(classes, edges, member_owners=owners)
            durations.append(time.perf_counter() - start)
            # Assertions execute outside the timed region.  Every answer follows
            # from the regular ring construction, independently of the detector.
            assert result.stats.unique_member_edges == count * degree
            assert result.stats.class_pairs == count * degree
            assert result.stats.duplicate_member_edges == 0
            assert len(result.removed_classes) == expected_removed
            assert all(
                score.fanin == degree
                and score.referenced_members == 1
                and abs(score.max_dedication - 1 / degree) < 1e-14
                and abs(score.usage_equality - expected_equality) < 1e-14
                and abs(score.cardinality - expected_equality) < 1e-14
                for score in result.scores
            )
        peak_native = resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss
        peak_mib = peak_native / (1024 * 1024 if sys.platform == "darwin" else 1024)
        cases.append({
            "classes": count,
            "distinct_member_references": len(edges),
            "class_pairs": result.stats.class_pairs,
            "runs_seconds": durations,
            "median_seconds": statistics.median(durations),
            "process_high_water_rss_mib": peak_mib,
            "expected_fanin": degree,
            "expected_referenced_members_per_target": 1,
            "expected_max_dedication": 1 / degree,
            "expected_usage_equality": expected_equality,
            "expected_cardinality": expected_equality,
            "expected_removed_classes": expected_removed,
            "observed_removed_classes": len(result.removed_classes),
            "all_expected_outputs_checked": True,
        })
        del result, classes, owners, edges

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "algorithm": "JNode-UE (Yano and Matsuo, 2020)",
        "measured_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "environment": {"python": platform.python_version(), "platform": platform.platform()},
        "construction": {
            "name": "directed indexed ring with fixed outgoing offsets",
            "edge_rule": "C_i#run()V -> C_((i+j) mod N)#accept()V, j=1..degree",
            "degree": degree,
            "randomness": "none",
            "thresholds": {"usage_equality": 0.85, "cardinality": 0.90},
        },
        "measurement_protocol": {
            "repeats": repeats,
            "summary": "median",
            "timed_region": "detect_omnipresent only, including input validation, diagnostics and output ordering",
            "excluded_from_timing": ["input creation", "garbage collection", "expected-output checks"],
            "memory": "process high-water RSS, cumulative across cases, including input and result objects",
            "previous_result_retained_until_next_call_completes": True,
            "limitations": "synthetic scaling evidence; excludes downloads, extraction and clustering; environment-dependent timing",
        },
        "code_sha256": {
            str(path.relative_to(ROOT)): hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            for path in (ROOT / "src/evolution/omnipresent.py", Path(__file__).resolve())
        },
        "cases": cases,
    }


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--sizes", default="1000,5000,10000", help="comma-separated class counts")
    parser.add_argument("--degree", type=int, default=20)
    parser.add_argument("--repeats", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=ROOT / "results/performance/ue_benchmark.json")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    data = run_benchmark([int(value) for value in args.sizes.split(",")], args.degree, args.repeats)
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
    for case in data["cases"]:
        print(f"{case['classes']} classes / {case['distinct_member_references']} references: "
              f"{case['median_seconds']:.6f} s; expected outputs verified")
    print(f"Saved {args.output}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run the paper-based JNode-UE replacement as a standalone explainable tool."""
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
from evolution.graph import read_csv, write_csv, write_json
from evolution.omnipresent import detect_omnipresent

if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--classes', type=Path, required=True, help='Extractor classes.csv, with class column')
    parser.add_argument('--members', type=Path, required=True, help='Extractor member_dependencies.csv')
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True, help='New output directory')
    parser.add_argument('--usage-threshold', type=float, default=.85)
    parser.add_argument('--cardinality-threshold', type=float, default=.90)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error('Choose a new output directory to preserve any previous run')
    classes = [r['class'] for r in read_csv(args.classes)]
    rows = read_csv(args.members)
    started = time.perf_counter()
    result = detect_omnipresent(classes, rows, usage_threshold=args.usage_threshold,
                                cardinality_threshold=args.cardinality_threshold)
    seconds = time.perf_counter() - started
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)
    write_json(args.output / 'ue.json', result.to_dict(include_dedications=True))
    write_csv(args.output / 'omnipresent.csv', [r.to_dict() for r in result.scores])
    write_csv(args.output / 'removed_classes.csv', [{'class': n} for n in result.removed_classes], ['class'])
    write_json(args.output / 'run.json', {'method': 'Yano and Matsuo2020 UE; not historical ComponentRank JNode',
                'classes_file': str(args.classes), 'members_file': str(args.members),
                'detector_seconds_excluding_csv_io': seconds, 'classes': len(classes),
                'removed': len(result.removed_classes)})
    print(f'JNode-UE: {len(result.removed_classes)} of {len(classes)} classes; {seconds:.6f}s detector-only')
src/evolution/omnipresent.py · 12,279 bytes

SHA-256: b8dea8f90a2c7c48757e87ac388c43ff97bc228450f99836a12c473dc107ac17

"""JNode-UE: member-aware omnipresent detection from Yano and Matsuo (2020).

This is the usage-equality (UE) method in equations 1--3, printed page 663,
of ``Moderate Detection and Removal of Omnipresent Modules in Software
Clustering``.  It is a replacement for, not an implementation of, the earlier
ComponentRank/shortest-path JNode algorithm compared with UE in that paper.

The input owners must already be canonical, outermost project classes.  Member
identifiers must retain their original declaring class, kind, and descriptor:
merging class owners must not merge distinct methods with the same signature.
Only distinct method/field member dependencies supply evidence for UE.  Other
class relations (such as inheritance) do not become fabricated member edges.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from fractions import Fraction
from math import fsum, isfinite, ulp
from typing import TypeAlias


MemberEdge: TypeAlias = tuple[str, str] | Mapping[str, str]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UEClassScore:
    """The complete decision for one normalized project class."""

    class_name: str
    fanin: int
    referenced_members: int
    max_dedication: float | None
    usage_equality: float | None
    cardinality: float | None
    strongest_caller: str | None
    usage_equality_passed: bool
    cardinality_passed: bool
    omnipresent: bool
    reason: str

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return asdict(self)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UEDedication:
    """Equation 1 for a distinct pair of owner classes."""

    source_class: str
    target_class: str
    referenced_members: int
    dedication: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UEStats:
    """Counts reconciling input rows with the scoped, deduplicated evidence."""

    classes: int
    input_member_edges: int
    excluded_external_edges: int
    excluded_intraclass_edges: int
    duplicate_member_edges: int
    unique_member_edges: int
    referenced_target_members: int
    class_pairs: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class UEResult:
    usage_threshold: float
    cardinality_threshold: float
    scores: tuple[UEClassScore, ...]
    dedications: tuple[UEDedication, ...]
    stats: UEStats

    @property
    def removed_classes(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        return tuple(score.class_name for score in self.scores if score.omnipresent)

    @property
    def retained_classes(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        return tuple(score.class_name for score in self.scores if not score.omnipresent)

    def by_class(self) -> dict[str, UEClassScore]:
        return {score.class_name: score for score in self.scores}

    def to_dict(self, *, include_dedications: bool = False) -> dict:
        """Return JSON-safe, deterministically ordered evidence.

        Per-class decisions always appear.  The optional pair table is useful
        when explaining an individual decision but can make release JSON large.
        """
        result = {
            "algorithm": "JNode-UE (Yano and Matsuo, 2020)",
            "scope": "resolved project member-use graph; normalized outermost owners",
            "usage_threshold": self.usage_threshold,
            "cardinality_threshold": self.cardinality_threshold,
            "removed_classes": list(self.removed_classes),
            "scores": [score.to_dict() for score in self.scores],
            "stats": asdict(self.stats),
        }
        if include_dedications:
            result["dedications"] = [asdict(pair) for pair in self.dedications]
        return result


def _threshold(value: float, name: str) -> tuple[float, Fraction]:
    numeric = float(value)
    if not isfinite(numeric) or not 0.0 <= numeric <= 1.0:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite and between 0 and 1 inclusive")
    # Treat the textual decimal threshold as the intended value.  In particular,
    # ceil(1 / (1 - 0.9)) with binary floats would incorrectly require 11 callers.
    return numeric, Fraction(str(value))


def _at_most_exact_boundary(
    value: float, denominators: list[int], cutoff: Fraction
) -> bool:
    """Compare a reciprocal sum without changing an inclusive boundary.

    Fast floating-point evaluation suffices away from the cutoff.  Only at a
    binary-rounding boundary do we sum exact rational values.  This is not an
    epsilon that changes the paper's decision rule.
    """
    limit = float(cutoff)
    rounding_margin = 8 * max(ulp(value), ulp(limit))
    if value < limit - rounding_margin:
        return True
    if value > limit + rounding_margin:
        return False
    return sum((Fraction(1, denominator) for denominator in denominators), Fraction()) <= cutoff


def detect_omnipresent(
    classes: Iterable[str],
    member_edges: Iterable[MemberEdge],
    *,
    member_owners: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    usage_threshold: float = 0.85,
    cardinality_threshold: float = 0.90,
) -> UEResult:
    """Detect all omnipresent classes once on the original scoped graph.

    ``member_edges`` accepts either dictionaries with ``source_class``,
    ``source_member``, ``target_class``, ``target_member``, or two-tuples of
    globally unique member IDs.  The tuple form requires ``member_owners``.
    Owners are normalized project class names.  A missing tuple owner is an
    input error; an explicitly out-of-scope owner is filtered as external.

    Exact member pairs and class-to-target-member pairs are deduplicated
    separately.  Two different methods of A calling the same B method increase
    that method's xm_fanin twice but contribute it only once to D_M(A, B).

    Hash aggregation is linear in the number of distinct member references,
    class pairs, and classes.  Deterministic output sorting additionally costs
    O(C log C + N log N), for C class pairs and N classes.  No dense graph,
    shortest-path enumeration, iteration, or post-removal recomputation occurs.
    """
    usage_float, usage_fraction = _threshold(usage_threshold, "usage_threshold")
    cardinality_float, cardinality_fraction = _threshold(
        cardinality_threshold, "cardinality_threshold"
    )
    class_set = set(classes)
    if any(not isinstance(name, str) or not name for name in class_set):
        raise ValueError("classes must contain nonempty class names")

    owners: dict[str, str] = {}
    source_members_by_target: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    caller_classes_by_target: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    target_members_by_class: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    input_count = excluded_external = excluded_intraclass = duplicate_count = 0

    for edge in member_edges:
        input_count += 1
        if isinstance(edge, Mapping):
            try:
                source = edge["source_member"]
                target = edge["target_member"]
                source_class = edge["source_class"]
                target_class = edge["target_class"]
            except KeyError as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"member edge is missing {exc.args[0]!r}") from exc
        else:
            if member_owners is None:
                raise ValueError("tuple member edges require a member_owners mapping")
            if isinstance(edge, (str, bytes)) or len(edge) != 2:
                raise ValueError("member edges must be pairs of member identifiers")
            source, target = edge
            try:
                source_class = member_owners[source]
                target_class = member_owners[target]
            except KeyError as exc:
                raise ValueError(f"owner missing for member {exc.args[0]!r}") from exc

        if any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in (source, target, source_class, target_class)):
            raise ValueError("member IDs and owner class names must be nonempty strings")
        if source_class not in class_set or target_class not in class_set:
            excluded_external += 1
            continue
        if source_class == target_class:
            excluded_intraclass += 1
            continue

        for member, owner in ((source, source_class), (target, target_class)):
            previous = owners.setdefault(member, owner)
            if previous != owner:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"member ID {member!r} has conflicting owners {previous!r} and {owner!r}; "
                    "use globally unique member identifiers"
                )

        callers = source_members_by_target[target]
        if source in callers:
            duplicate_count += 1
            continue
        callers.add(source)
        caller_classes_by_target[target].add(source_class)
        target_members_by_class[target_class].add(target)

    # Each target member contributes once for each distinct CALLER CLASS;
    # the denominator still counts all distinct caller MEMBERS outside B.
    pair_denominators: dict[tuple[str, str], list[int]] = defaultdict(list)
    callers_by_class: dict[str, set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
    for target, caller_members in source_members_by_target.items():
        target_class = owners[target]
        denominator = len(caller_members) * len(target_members_by_class[target_class])
        for caller_class in caller_classes_by_target[target]:
            pair_denominators[caller_class, target_class].append(denominator)
            callers_by_class[target_class].add(caller_class)

    maximum: dict[str, float] = {}
    strongest: dict[str, str] = {}
    equality_passed: dict[str, bool] = defaultdict(lambda: True)
    dedication_rows: list[UEDedication] = []
    for (source_class, target_class), denominators in sorted(pair_denominators.items()):
        dedication = fsum(1 / denominator for denominator in denominators)
        dedication_rows.append(
            UEDedication(source_class, target_class, len(denominators), dedication)
        )
        if target_class not in maximum or dedication > maximum[target_class]:
            maximum[target_class] = dedication
            strongest[target_class] = source_class
        if not _at_most_exact_boundary(dedication, denominators, 1 - usage_fraction):
            equality_passed[target_class] = False

    score_rows: list[UEClassScore] = []
    for class_name in sorted(class_set):
        fanin = len(callers_by_class.get(class_name, ()))
        member_count = len(target_members_by_class.get(class_name, ()))
        if fanin == 0:
            # The paper leaves max(empty) and 1/0 undefined.  Keep the class;
            # no outside member-use evidence cannot establish omnipresence.
            score_rows.append(
                UEClassScore(class_name, 0, 0, None, None, None, None, False, False, False,
                             "no_external_member_use")
            )
            continue
        max_dedication = maximum[class_name]
        equality = 1.0 - max_dedication
        cardinality = 1.0 - 1.0 / fanin
        passes_e = equality_passed[class_name]
        passes_c = fanin * (1 - cardinality_fraction) >= 1
        is_omnipresent = passes_e and passes_c
        if not passes_e:
            reason = "usage_equality_below_threshold"
        elif not passes_c:
            reason = "cardinality_below_threshold"
        else:
            reason = "usage_equality_and_cardinality_at_or_above_thresholds"
        score_rows.append(
            UEClassScore(class_name, fanin, member_count, max_dedication, equality, cardinality,
                         strongest[class_name], passes_e, passes_c, is_omnipresent, reason)
        )

    return UEResult(
        usage_float,
        cardinality_float,
        tuple(score_rows),
        tuple(dedication_rows),
        UEStats(
            classes=len(class_set),
            input_member_edges=input_count,
            excluded_external_edges=excluded_external,
            excluded_intraclass_edges=excluded_intraclass,
            duplicate_member_edges=duplicate_count,
            unique_member_edges=sum(len(sources) for sources in source_members_by_target.values()),
            referenced_target_members=len(source_members_by_target),
            class_pairs=len(pair_denominators),
        ),
    )
tests/test_omnipresent.py · 11,240 bytes

SHA-256: 170c1ffb84493da581981463be3e2d4828ac79e2a9fd5fbc2f02bad4e6bd2154

"""Meaningful UE checks: paper equations, exact thresholds, and scope integrity."""

from fractions import Fraction
import json
import random
import unittest

from evolution.omnipresent import detect_omnipresent


def row(source_class, source_member, target_class, target_member):
    return {
        "source_class": source_class,
        "source_member": f"{source_class}#{source_member}",
        "target_class": target_class,
        "target_member": f"{target_class}#{target_member}",
    }


def exact_paper_oracle(classes, rows, usage=Fraction(17, 20), cardinality=Fraction(9, 10)):
    """Deliberately slow set definitions and Fraction arithmetic from Eq. 1--3.

    This independently expresses the mathematical sets instead of copying the
    optimized implementation's aggregation steps.
    """
    classes = set(classes)
    edges = {
        (r["source_class"], r["source_member"], r["target_class"], r["target_member"])
        for r in rows
        if r["source_class"] in classes
        and r["target_class"] in classes
        and r["source_class"] != r["target_class"]
    }
    result = {}
    for target_class in classes:
        incoming = {edge for edge in edges if edge[2] == target_class}
        callers = {edge[0] for edge in incoming}
        targets = {edge[3] for edge in incoming}
        if not callers:
            result[target_class] = (0, 0, None, None, None, False)
            continue
        dedication = {}
        for caller in callers:
            used = {edge[3] for edge in incoming if edge[0] == caller}
            dedication[caller] = sum(
                (
                    Fraction(1, len({edge[1] for edge in incoming if edge[3] == member}) * len(targets))
                    for member in used
                ),
                Fraction(),
            )
        maximum = max(dedication.values())
        equality = 1 - maximum
        c = 1 - Fraction(1, len(callers))
        result[target_class] = (
            len(callers), len(targets), maximum, equality, c,
            equality >= usage and c >= cardinality,
        )
    return result


class TestUsageEquality(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_and_isolated_classes_have_no_invented_evidence(self):
        self.assertEqual(detect_omnipresent([], []).removed_classes, ())
        result = detect_omnipresent(["Alone"], [])
        score = result.by_class()["Alone"]
        self.assertEqual(score.reason, "no_external_member_use")
        self.assertIsNone(score.usage_equality)
        self.assertIsNone(score.cardinality)
        self.assertEqual(result.retained_classes, ("Alone",))

    def test_cardinality_boundary_is_ten_callers_not_eleven(self):
        for count, expected in [(9, False), (10, True), (11, True)]:
            with self.subTest(count=count):
                callers = [f"C{i}" for i in range(count)]
                edges = [row(caller, "run()", "Utility", "log()") for caller in callers]
                score = detect_omnipresent(callers + ["Utility"], edges).by_class()["Utility"]
                self.assertEqual(score.fanin, count)
                self.assertEqual(score.omnipresent, expected)
                self.assertAlmostEqual(score.max_dedication, 1 / count)

    def test_usage_threshold_is_inclusive_at_exact_point_eighty_five(self):
        callers = [f"C{i}" for i in range(10)]
        edges = [row(caller, "run()", "Utility", "common()") for caller in callers]
        edges += [row(caller, "run()", "Utility", "less_common()") for caller in callers[:5]]
        # mx=2.  Five callers receive 1/(10*2)+1/(5*2)=3/20,
        # so e=17/20=.85 exactly, while c=.90 exactly.
        score = detect_omnipresent(callers + ["Utility"], edges).by_class()["Utility"]
        self.assertTrue(score.omnipresent)
        self.assertTrue(score.usage_equality_passed)
        self.assertTrue(score.cardinality_passed)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.usage_equality, 0.85)

    def test_source_members_are_counted_but_target_members_are_sets(self):
        edges = [
            row("A", "first()", "B", "value:I"),
            row("A", "second()", "B", "value:I"),
            row("C", "run()", "B", "value:I"),
        ]
        result = detect_omnipresent(["A", "B", "C"], edges + [edges[0]] * 4)
        score = result.by_class()["B"]
        self.assertEqual(score.fanin, 2)
        self.assertEqual(score.referenced_members, 1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.max_dedication, 1 / 3)
        self.assertEqual({p.source_class: p.dedication for p in result.dedications}, {"A": 1 / 3, "C": 1 / 3})
        self.assertEqual(result.stats.unique_member_edges, 3)
        self.assertEqual(result.stats.duplicate_member_edges, 4)

    def test_rare_setup_method_protects_a_popular_class(self):
        callers = [f"C{i}" for i in range(20)] + ["Startup"]
        edges = [row(caller, "run()", "Logger", "log()") for caller in callers]
        edges.append(row("Startup", "run()", "Logger", "init()"))
        score = detect_omnipresent(callers + ["Logger"], edges).by_class()["Logger"]
        self.assertEqual(score.fanin, 21)
        self.assertTrue(score.cardinality_passed)
        self.assertEqual(score.strongest_caller, "Startup")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.max_dedication, Fraction(1, 2) + Fraction(1, 42))
        self.assertFalse(score.omnipresent)
        self.assertEqual(score.reason, "usage_equality_below_threshold")

    def test_external_library_endpoints_do_not_enter_any_count(self):
        edges = [
            row("A", "run()", "Utility", "log()"),
            row("External", "run()", "Utility", "log()"),
            row("A", "run()", "java.lang.String", "length()"),
        ]
        result = detect_omnipresent(["A", "Utility"], edges)
        score = result.by_class()["Utility"]
        self.assertEqual(result.stats.excluded_external_edges, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result.stats.unique_member_edges, 1)
        self.assertEqual(score.max_dedication, 1.0)
        self.assertEqual(score.fanin, 1)

    def test_merged_inner_owners_keep_distinct_original_member_ids(self):
        edges = [
            {"source_class": "Outer", "source_member": "Outer$Inner#run()V",
             "target_class": "Utility", "target_member": "Utility#log()V"},
            {"source_class": "Outer", "source_member": "Outer$1#run()V",
             "target_class": "Utility", "target_member": "Utility#log()V"},
            row("Consumer", "run()", "Utility", "log()V"),
            {"source_class": "Outer", "source_member": "Outer$1#run()V",
             "target_class": "Outer", "target_member": "Outer#accept()V"},
        ]
        result = detect_omnipresent(["Outer", "Consumer", "Utility"], edges)
        score = result.by_class()["Utility"]
        self.assertEqual(result.stats.excluded_intraclass_edges, 1)
        self.assertEqual(score.fanin, 2)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.max_dedication, 1 / 3)

    def test_tuple_interface_and_unreferenced_members(self):
        owners = {"A#run()": "A", "B#used:I": "B", "B#unused:I": "B"}
        result = detect_omnipresent(["A", "B"], [("A#run()", "B#used:I")], member_owners=owners)
        score = result.by_class()["B"]
        self.assertEqual(score.referenced_members, 1)
        self.assertEqual(score.max_dedication, 1.0)

    def test_removal_is_simultaneous_without_iterative_recalculation(self):
        callers = [f"C{i}" for i in range(9)]
        edges = [row(caller, "run()", target, "use()") for caller in callers for target in ("U", "V")]
        edges += [row("U", "run()", "V", "use()"), row("V", "run()", "U", "use()")]
        result = detect_omnipresent(callers + ["U", "V"], edges)
        self.assertEqual(result.removed_classes, ("U", "V"))

    def test_serialization_is_deterministic_and_json_safe(self):
        callers = [f"C{i}" for i in range(12)]
        edges = [row(caller, "run()", "U", f"use{index % 3}()") for index, caller in enumerate(callers)]
        first = detect_omnipresent(callers + ["U"], edges)
        rng = random.Random(23)
        rng.shuffle(edges)
        second = detect_omnipresent(list(reversed(callers)) + ["U"], edges)
        self.assertEqual(first.to_dict(include_dedications=True), second.to_dict(include_dedications=True))
        json.dumps(first.to_dict(include_dedications=True), allow_nan=False)

    def test_optimized_result_matches_independent_exact_oracle(self):
        for seed in range(12):
            with self.subTest(seed=seed):
                rng = random.Random(seed)
                classes = [f"C{i}" for i in range(14)]
                edges = [
                    row(rng.choice(classes), f"m{rng.randrange(3)}()",
                        rng.choice(classes), f"m{rng.randrange(3)}()")
                    for _ in range(550)
                ]
                edges += edges[:15]
                edges += [row("third.party.Library", "run()", "C0", "m0()")]
                expected = exact_paper_oracle(classes, edges)
                result = detect_omnipresent(classes, edges).by_class()
                for cls, (fanin, mx, maximum, equality, cardinality, removed) in expected.items():
                    score = result[cls]
                    self.assertEqual(score.fanin, fanin)
                    self.assertEqual(score.referenced_members, mx)
                    self.assertEqual(score.omnipresent, removed)
                    if maximum is None:
                        self.assertIsNone(score.max_dedication)
                    else:
                        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.max_dedication, float(maximum), places=14)
                        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.usage_equality, float(equality), places=14)
                        self.assertAlmostEqual(score.cardinality, float(cardinality), places=14)

    def test_input_errors_do_not_silently_remove_evidence(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "require a member_owners"):
            detect_omnipresent(["A", "B"], [("A#m", "B#m")])
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "owner missing"):
            detect_omnipresent(["A", "B"], [("A#m", "B#m")], member_owners={"A#m": "A"})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "missing"):
            detect_omnipresent(["A", "B"], [{"source_class": "A"}])
        conflicting = [row("A", "run()", "U", "use()"), row("B", "run()", "U", "use()")]
        conflicting[1]["source_member"] = conflicting[0]["source_member"]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "conflicting owners"):
            detect_omnipresent(["A", "B", "U"], conflicting)
        for invalid in (-0.1, 1.1, float("nan"), float("inf")):
            with self.subTest(invalid=invalid), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                detect_omnipresent([], [], usage_threshold=invalid)

    def test_zero_and_one_thresholds_keep_empty_evidence_safe(self):
        edges = [row("A", "run()", "B", "use()")]
        low = detect_omnipresent(["A", "B", "Isolated"], edges, usage_threshold=0, cardinality_threshold=0)
        self.assertEqual(low.removed_classes, ("B",))
        high = detect_omnipresent(["A", "B"], edges, usage_threshold=1, cardinality_threshold=1)
        self.assertEqual(high.removed_classes, ())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
