#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Acquire the pinned Guava dataset without executing upstream software.

Normal use: python scripts/fetch_data.py
Offline check: python scripts/fetch_data.py --offline
Repeat endpoint source provenance: python scripts/fetch_data.py --verify-git

--bootstrap is only for the initial collection: it fills absent SHA-256 pins
after validating Maven's SHA-1 sidecars, and never replaces an existing pin.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
import zipfile
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET

from select_versions import validate_manifest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
USER_AGENT = "EPL484-Software-Evolution-Reproducible-Research/1.0"
JAVA_COMMENTS = re.compile(
    r'//[^\n]*|/\*.*?\*/|"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|\'(?:\\.|[^\'\\])*\'',
    re.S,
)


def utc_now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")


def digest(data, algorithm="sha256"):
    return hashlib.new(algorithm, data).hexdigest()


def write_json(path, value):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    temporary.replace(path)


def download(url, timeout=40):
    """HTTPS-only public download with bounded retries and response size."""
    if not url.startswith("https://"):
        raise ValueError(f"Expected HTTPS URL: {url}")
    for attempt in range(3):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": USER_AGENT})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
                data = response.read(100_000_001)
            if len(data) > 100_000_000:
                raise ValueError(f"Unexpectedly large response: {url}")
            return data
        except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError):
            if attempt == 2:
                raise
            time.sleep(1 + attempt)
    raise AssertionError("unreachable")


def validate_selection(manifest):
    inventory = json.loads((ROOT / "data/provenance/tag_inventory.json").read_text())
    validate_manifest(manifest, inventory)


def artifact_file(row, kind):
    return ROOT / row[f"{kind}_path"]


def fetch_artifact(row, kind, offline, bootstrap):
    path = artifact_file(row, kind)
    expected = row.get(f"{kind}_sha256")
    if not expected and not bootstrap:
        raise ValueError(f"Unpinned {kind} for {row['version']}; initial collection needs --bootstrap.")
    from_cache = path.exists()
    if from_cache:
        data = path.read_bytes()
    elif offline:
        raise FileNotFoundError(path)
    else:
        data = download(row[f"{kind}_url"])
    sha256 = digest(data)
    if expected and sha256 != expected:
        raise ValueError(f"SHA-256 mismatch: {path}; cached files are never silently overwritten.")

    if kind in ("jar", "sources"):
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as archive:
            bad = archive.testzip()
            if bad:
                raise ValueError(f"Corrupt ZIP member {bad} in {path}")
            required_suffix = ".class" if kind == "jar" else ".java"
            if not any(n.endswith(required_suffix) for n in archive.namelist()):
                raise ValueError(f"No {required_suffix} members in {path}")
    else:
        doc = ET.fromstring(data)
        ns = {"m": "http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"}
        version = doc.findtext("m:version", namespaces=ns) or doc.findtext("m:parent/m:version", namespaces=ns)
        artifact = doc.findtext("m:artifactId", namespaces=ns)
        if version != row.get("artifact_version", row["version"]) or artifact != row["artifact_id"]:
            raise ValueError(f"Maven identity mismatch: {path}")

    published_sha1 = row.get(f"{kind}_maven_sha1")
    if published_sha1:
        if digest(data, "sha1") != published_sha1:
            raise ValueError(f"Pinned Maven SHA-1 mismatch: {path}")
    elif not offline:
        # Maven Central published SHA-1 sidecars exist for the historical releases.
        sidecar = download(row[f"{kind}_url"] + ".sha1").decode().strip()
        match = re.search(r"\b[a-fA-F0-9]{40}\b", sidecar)
        if not match:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid Maven SHA-1 sidecar: {path}")
        published_sha1 = match.group(0).lower()
        if digest(data, "sha1") != published_sha1:
            raise ValueError(f"Published Maven SHA-1 mismatch: {path}")

    if not from_cache:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".part")
        temporary.write_bytes(data)
        temporary.replace(path)
    return {
        "version": row["version"], "kind": kind, "path": row[f"{kind}_path"],
        "url": row[f"{kind}_url"], "bytes": len(data), "sha256": sha256,
        "maven_sha1": published_sha1, "cached": from_cache,
    }


def source_inventory(row):
    """Source SLOC, raw bytecode counts, and source-to-binary ownership evidence."""
    records = []
    prefixes = tuple(row.get("source_prefixes", [row["source_prefix"]]))
    with zipfile.ZipFile(artifact_file(row, "sources")) as archive:
        for name in sorted(archive.namelist()):
            if not name.endswith(".java"):
                continue
            text = archive.read(name).decode("utf-8")
            clean = JAVA_COMMENTS.sub(
                lambda m: "\n" * m.group(0).count("\n")
                if m.group(0).startswith(("//", "/*")) else m.group(0),
                text,
            )
            records.append({
                "path": name,
                "sha256": digest(archive.read(name)),
                "physical_lines": len(text.splitlines()),
                "nonblank_noncomment_lines": sum(bool(s.strip()) for s in clean.splitlines()),
            })
    owned_sources = {r["path"][:-5] for r in records
                     if r["path"].startswith(prefixes)
                     and not r["path"].endswith("package-info.java")}
    with zipfile.ZipFile(artifact_file(row, "jar")) as archive:
        all_classes = [s for s in archive.namelist() if s.endswith(".class")]
        base_classes = [s for s in all_classes if not s.startswith("META-INF/versions/")]
        unsupported = []
        owned = []
        for name in base_classes:
            outer = name[:-6].split("$", 1)[0]
            if outer in owned_sources:
                owned.append(name)
            elif not name.endswith(("module-info.class", "package-info.class")):
                unsupported.append(name)
    return {
        "version": row["version"], "source_java_files": len(records),
        "source_ownership_prefixes": list(prefixes),
        "source_owned_java_files": sum(r["path"].startswith(prefixes) for r in records),
        "source_owned_java_nonblank_noncomment_lines": sum(r["nonblank_noncomment_lines"] for r in records if r["path"].startswith(prefixes)),
        "java_physical_lines": sum(r["physical_lines"] for r in records),
        "java_nonblank_noncomment_lines": sum(r["nonblank_noncomment_lines"] for r in records),
        "raw_class_entries_including_multi_release": len(all_classes),
        "base_class_entries": len(base_classes),
        "source_owned_base_class_entries": len(owned),
        "base_classes_without_owned_source": unsupported,
        "source_files": records,
        "sloc_definition": "Physical Java lines with noncomment, nonwhitespace content. "
            "A lexical scanner preserves string/character literals and removes line/block comments. "
            "Includes source JAR package/module descriptors; not a logical statement count.",
    }


def compare_git_sources(row, repository, offline):
    """Compare every source-JAR Java file with the immutable tag commit ZIP."""
    commit = row["commit_sha"]
    url = f"https://codeload.github.com/{repository}/zip/{commit}"
    cache = ROOT / "data" / "upstream_git" / f"{commit}.zip"
    if cache.exists():
        data = cache.read_bytes()
    elif offline:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Git ZIP unavailable for offline check: {cache}")
    else:
        data = download(url)
        cache.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        cache.write_bytes(data)
    records = []
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as git_zip, \
            zipfile.ZipFile(artifact_file(row, "sources")) as sources:
        # codeload ZIP starts with one repository-and-commit directory.
        git_files = {
            name.split("/", 1)[1]: name
            for name in git_zip.namelist() if "/" in name and not name.endswith("/")
        }
        for name in sorted(sources.namelist()):
            if not name.endswith(".java"):
                continue
            observed = sources.read(name)
            git_paths = [root + name for root in row.get("source_roots", ["src/main/java/"])]
            git_path = next((p for p in git_paths if p in git_files), git_paths[0])
            if git_path in git_files:
                original = git_zip.read(git_files[git_path])
                status = "byte_identical" if observed == original else "mismatch"
                if status == "mismatch" and observed.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n") == original.replace(b"\r\n", b"\n"):
                    status = "line_endings_only"
                record = {"source_path": name, "git_path": git_path,
                          "status": status, "source_sha256": digest(observed),
                          "git_sha256": digest(original)}
            else:
                record = {"source_path": name, "git_path": git_path,
                          "status": "missing_from_git", "source_sha256": digest(observed)}
            records.append(record)
    failures = [r for r in records if r["status"] in ("mismatch", "missing_from_git")]
    evidence = {
        "version": row["version"], "commit_sha": commit, "git_archive_url": url,
        "git_archive_sha256": digest(data), "source_jar_sha256": row["sources_sha256"],
        "compared_java_files": len(records), "all_sources_match": not failures,
        "records": records,
    }
    write_json(ROOT / "data/provenance" / f"git_source_match_{row['version']}.json", evidence)
    if failures:
        raise ValueError(f"Git/Maven source differences for {row['version']}: {failures[:5]}")
    return {k: v for k, v in evidence.items() if k != "records"}


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=ROOT / "config/versions.json")
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--offline", action="store_true", help="Only verify checked-in cached Maven artifacts")
    parser.add_argument("--bootstrap", action="store_true", help="Fill initially absent checksum pins after collection")
    parser.add_argument("--verify-git", action="store_true", help="Compare oldest/latest source JARs to immutable Git commits")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    manifest = json.loads(args.manifest.read_text())
    validate_selection(manifest)
    if not 1 <= args.workers <= 8:
        parser.error("--workers must be between 1 and 8")
    started = utc_now()
    artifacts = []
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:
        pending = {pool.submit(fetch_artifact, row, kind, args.offline, args.bootstrap): (row, kind)
                   for row in manifest["versions"] for kind in ("jar", "sources", "pom")}
        for future in as_completed(pending):
            result = future.result()
            artifacts.append(result)
            row, kind = pending[future]
            row[f"{kind}_sha256"] = result["sha256"]
            row[f"{kind}_maven_sha1"] = result["maven_sha1"]
    if args.bootstrap:
        write_json(args.manifest, manifest)
    checksum_lines = [f"{row[f'{kind}_sha256']}  {row[f'{kind}_path']}"
                      for row in manifest["versions"] for kind in ("jar", "sources", "pom")]
    checksum_path = ROOT / "data/provenance/SHA256SUMS"
    checksum_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    checksum_path.write_text("\n".join(checksum_lines) + "\n")
    inventories = []
    for row in manifest["versions"]:
        info = source_inventory(row)
        inventories.append({k: v for k, v in info.items() if k != "source_files"})
        write_json(ROOT / "data/provenance" / f"source_inventory_{row['version']}.json", info)
    git_checks = []
    if args.verify_git:
        for row in (manifest["versions"][0], manifest["versions"][-1]):
            git_checks.append(compare_git_sources(row, manifest["repository"], args.offline))
    previous_path = ROOT / "data/provenance/acquisition.json"
    previous = json.loads(previous_path.read_text()) if previous_path.exists() else {}
    artifact_order = {v["version"]: i for i, v in enumerate(manifest["versions"])}
    artifacts.sort(key=lambda r: (artifact_order[r["version"]], r["kind"]))
    report = {
        "started_at_utc": started, "completed_at_utc": utc_now(),
        "mode": "offline_verify" if args.offline else "download_or_verify",
        "repository": manifest["repository"], "versions": len(manifest["versions"]),
        "study_revision": manifest.get("study_revision", 1),
        "selected_versions": [row["version"] for row in manifest["versions"]],
        "selected_unique_families": len({row.get("family", ".".join(row["version"].split(".")[:2]))
                                          for row in manifest["versions"]}),
        "selection_manifest_sha256": digest(args.manifest.read_bytes()),
        "artifact_count": len(artifacts), "total_artifact_bytes": sum(r["bytes"] for r in artifacts),
        "all_pinned_sha256_verified": True, "artifacts": artifacts,
        "source_inventories": inventories,
        "git_endpoint_source_checks": git_checks or previous.get("git_endpoint_source_checks", []),
    }
    write_json(previous_path, report)
    latest = inventories[-1]
    print(json.dumps({
        "versions": report["versions"], "verified_artifacts": len(artifacts),
        "total_mib": round(report["total_artifact_bytes"] / 1024**2, 3),
        "latest": latest["version"],
        "latest_source_java_files": latest["source_java_files"],
        "latest_java_nonblank_noncomment_lines": latest["java_nonblank_noncomment_lines"],
        "latest_source_owned_java_sloc": latest["source_owned_java_nonblank_noncomment_lines"],
        "git_endpoint_source_checks": report["git_endpoint_source_checks"],
    }, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package complete raw and computed data without discarding any study evidence."""
from __future__ import annotations
import base64
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import tempfile
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PART_BYTES = 512 * 1024


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def store_base64(bundle: dict, content: bytes) -> dict:
    """Store the exact logical ZIP as canonical, unwrapped ASCII base64."""
    if len(content) != bundle['bytes'] or hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest() != bundle['sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Logical ZIP identity differs before base64 storage')
    relative = PurePosixPath(bundle['path'])
    if relative.is_absolute() or '..' in relative.parts or relative.as_posix() != bundle['path'] or relative.parts[0] != 'archives' or relative.suffix != '.zip' or '\\' in bundle['path']:
        raise ValueError(f'Invalid logical ZIP path: {bundle["path"]}')
    physical = ROOT / (bundle['path'] + '.b64')
    physical.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    encoded = base64.b64encode(content)
    encoded_sha = hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()
    if not physical.exists() or digest(physical) != encoded_sha:
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(prefix='encoded-stage-', dir=physical.parent, delete=False) as stream:
            temporary = Path(stream.name)
            stream.write(encoded)
        try:
            temporary.replace(physical)
        finally:
            temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    record = {key: value for key, value in bundle.items()
              if key not in ('storage', 'part_size_bytes', 'parts', 'encoded_file')}
    record.update(storage='base64', encoded_file={
        'path': physical.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(), 'bytes': len(encoded),
        'sha256': encoded_sha, 'text_encoding': 'ascii'})
    return record


def pack(name: str, paths: list[Path], *, multipart: bool | None = None) -> dict:
    relative_name = PurePosixPath(name)
    if relative_name.is_absolute() or '..' in relative_name.parts or str(relative_name) != name or '\\' in name:
        raise ValueError(f'Invalid bundle name: {name}')
    target = ROOT / 'archives' / name
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    members = []
    seen = set()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='package-stage-', dir=target.parent) as directory:
        stage = Path(directory)
        temporary = stage / 'complete.zip'
        with zipfile.ZipFile(temporary, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=6) as archive:
            for path in sorted(paths):
                if not path.is_file() or '__pycache__' in path.parts or path.name.endswith('.part'):
                    continue
                relative = path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
                if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()) or path.is_symlink():
                    raise ValueError(f'Source is not an ordinary repository file: {path}')
                if relative in seen:
                    raise ValueError(f'Duplicate archive member: {relative}')
                seen.add(relative)
                content = path.read_bytes()
                info = zipfile.ZipInfo(relative, date_time=(2026, 10, 17, 0, 0, 0))
                info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
                info.external_attr = 0o100644 << 16
                archive.writestr(info, content, compress_type=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=6)
                members.append({'path': relative, 'bytes': len(content), 'sha256': hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()})
        record = {'path': target.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(), 'bytes': temporary.stat().st_size,
                  'sha256': digest(temporary), 'member_count': len(members), 'members': members}
        if multipart is False:
            record['storage'] = 'whole'
            if not target.exists() or digest(target) != record['sha256']:
                temporary.replace(target)
            return record
        if multipart is None:
            return store_base64(record, temporary.read_bytes())
        record.update({'storage': 'multipart', 'part_size_bytes': PART_BYTES, 'parts': []})
        with temporary.open('rb') as stream:
            index = 1
            while content := stream.read(PART_BYTES):
                physical = target.with_name(f'{target.name}.{record["sha256"]}.part{index:04d}')
                staged = stage / physical.name
                staged.write_bytes(content)
                part = {'index': index, 'path': physical.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
                        'bytes': len(content), 'sha256': hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()}
                record['parts'].append(part)
                index += 1
        # All parts are complete before publishing any. Hash-qualified paths
        # preserve the previous generation until the new manifest is installed.
        for part in record['parts']:
            physical = ROOT / part['path']
            staged = stage / physical.name
            if not physical.exists() or digest(physical) != part['sha256']:
                staged.replace(physical)
        return record


def obsolete_bundles(expected_names: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    """Catch superseded whole ZIPs, numbered parts, and unfinished old ZIPs."""
    return sorted(p.name for p in (ROOT / 'archives/versions').glob('guava-*.zip*')
                  if p.name.split('.zip', 1)[0] + '.zip' not in expected_names)


def remove_superseded_storage(bundles: list[dict]) -> None:
    """Run only after the replacement manifest has been atomically installed."""
    for bundle in bundles:
        if bundle['storage'] not in ('multipart', 'base64'):
            continue
        logical = ROOT / bundle['path']
        keep = {ROOT / part['path'] for part in bundle.get('parts', [])}
        for old in logical.parent.glob(logical.name + '.*part*'):
            if old.is_file() and old not in keep:
                old.unlink()
        if logical.exists():
            logical.unlink()


def main() -> None:
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / 'config/versions.json').read_text())
    expected_names = {f"guava-{r['version']}.zip" for r in manifest['versions']}
    obsolete = obsolete_bundles(expected_names)
    if obsolete:
        raise ValueError(f'Archive superseded release bundles before packaging the revised cohort: {obsolete}')
    bundles = []
    for release in manifest['versions']:
        version = release['version']
        paths = []
        for prefix in ('data/raw', 'data/graphs', 'results/versions'):
            directory = ROOT / prefix / version
            if not directory.is_dir():
                raise FileNotFoundError(directory)
            paths.extend(directory.rglob('*'))
        record = pack(f'versions/guava-{version}.zip', paths)
        record['version'] = version
        bundles.append(record)
        print(version, record['member_count'], record['bytes'], flush=True)
    bundles.append(pack('pilot.zip', list((ROOT / 'results/pilot').rglob('*'))))
    git_archives = sorted((ROOT / 'data/upstream_git').glob('*.zip'))
    if len(git_archives) != 2:
        raise ValueError('Expected the first and last immutable Git source archives')
    bundles.append(pack('upstream_git.zip', git_archives, multipart=False))
    payload = {'schema_version': 3,
               'format': 'Logical standard ZIP/deflate; fixed timestamps; complete original file bytes; version and pilot ZIPs stored as canonical padded RFC4648 base64 ASCII without whitespace in .zip.b64 files',
               'purpose': 'All 90 pinned acquired artifacts and every finalized per-release computed file are preserved, including instructor raw output, unchanged scoped input JARs, supplementary member evidence, algorithm logs and directional MoJoFM details. The pilot bundle includes every k candidate assignment. Unfinished .part files are excluded. Unpack to restore the documented working hierarchy.',
               'version_manifest_sha256': digest(ROOT / 'config/versions.json'),
               'bundles': bundles}
    temporary_manifest = ROOT / 'archives/manifest.json.tmp'
    temporary_manifest.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + '\n')
    temporary_manifest.replace(ROOT / 'archives/manifest.json')
    remove_superseded_storage(bundles)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
scripts/unpack_data.py · 8,560 bytes

SHA-256: 8d765df3fbf89a03e83ed7dedc7632727f1c86b122340538fba32453438c56d0

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify and restore all preserved study data, or one selected release."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import base64
import binascii
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import stat
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PART_BYTES = 512 * 1024


def sha(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def safe_path(name: str, allowed_roots: tuple[str, ...]) -> Path:
    """Reject traversal, alternate separators, and filesystem symlink escapes."""
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or '\0' in name:
        raise ValueError(f'Invalid archive path: {name!r}')
    relative = PurePosixPath(name)
    if relative.is_absolute() or '..' in relative.parts or relative.as_posix() != name or len(relative.parts) < 2 or relative.parts[0] not in allowed_roots:
        raise ValueError(f'Invalid archive path: {name}')
    target = ROOT
    for piece in relative.parts:
        target /= piece
        if target.is_symlink():
            raise ValueError(f'Symlink in archive path: {name}')
    if not target.resolve().is_relative_to(ROOT.resolve()):
        raise ValueError(f'Archive path escapes repository: {name}')
    return target


def logical_zip_bytes(bundle: dict) -> bytes:
    logical = safe_path(bundle['path'], ('archives',))
    if logical.suffix != '.zip' or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', bundle['sha256']):
        raise ValueError(f'Invalid logical ZIP record: {bundle["path"]}')
    storage = bundle.get('storage', 'whole')
    if storage == 'whole':
        if bundle.get('parts') or bundle.get('encoded_file'):
            raise ValueError('Whole ZIP record cannot also declare another physical storage')
        content = logical.read_bytes()
    elif storage == 'base64':
        encoded_file = bundle.get('encoded_file')
        if not isinstance(encoded_file, dict) or bundle.get('parts') or bundle.get('part_size_bytes'):
            raise ValueError('Base64 ZIP requires a single encoded file record')
        physical = safe_path(encoded_file['path'], ('archives',))
        expected_size = 4 * ((bundle['bytes'] + 2) // 3)
        if encoded_file['path'] != bundle['path'] + '.b64' or encoded_file.get('text_encoding') != 'ascii' or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', encoded_file['sha256']):
            raise ValueError('Invalid encoded ZIP path or encoding record')
        encoded = physical.read_bytes()
        if encoded_file['bytes'] != expected_size or len(encoded) != expected_size or sha(encoded) != encoded_file['sha256']:
            raise ValueError(f'Encoded ZIP checksum/size mismatch: {encoded_file["path"]}')
        try:
            content = base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)
        except (ValueError, binascii.Error) as error:
            raise ValueError('Invalid strict base64 ZIP content') from error
        if base64.b64encode(content) != encoded:
            raise ValueError('Base64 ZIP content is not canonical padded RFC4648')
    elif storage == 'multipart':
        if bundle.get('encoded_file'):
            raise ValueError('Multipart ZIP cannot also declare an encoded file')
        if bundle.get('part_size_bytes') != PART_BYTES or not bundle.get('parts'):
            raise ValueError('Multipart ZIP requires ordered 512KiB parts')
        expected_count = (bundle['bytes'] + PART_BYTES - 1) // PART_BYTES
        if len(bundle['parts']) != expected_count:
            raise ValueError('Physical part count does not match logical ZIP size')
        pieces = []
        for index, part in enumerate(bundle['parts'], 1):
            physical = safe_path(part['path'], ('archives',))
            expected_path = f'{bundle["path"]}.{bundle["sha256"]}.part{index:04d}'
            if part['index'] != index or part['path'] != expected_path:
                raise ValueError(f'Physical part order/path mismatch: {part["path"]}')
            expected_bytes = min(PART_BYTES, bundle['bytes'] - (index - 1) * PART_BYTES)
            piece = physical.read_bytes()
            if part['bytes'] != expected_bytes or len(piece) != expected_bytes or sha(piece) != part['sha256']:
                raise ValueError(f'Physical part checksum/size mismatch: {part["path"]}')
            pieces.append(piece)
        content = b''.join(pieces)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f'Unknown archive storage: {storage}')
    if len(content) != bundle['bytes'] or sha(content) != bundle['sha256']:
        raise ValueError(f'Logical ZIP checksum/size mismatch: {bundle["path"]}')
    return content


def checked_members(bundle: dict):
    """Validate the complete ZIP inventory; yield verified file bytes only."""
    expected = {member['path']: member for member in bundle['members']}
    if len(expected) != len(bundle['members']) or bundle['member_count'] != len(expected):
        raise ValueError(f'Duplicate/mismatched manifest members: {bundle["path"]}')
    for name in expected:
        safe_path(name, ('data', 'results'))
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(logical_zip_bytes(bundle))) as archive:
        if len(archive.namelist()) != len(expected) or set(archive.namelist()) != set(expected):
            raise ValueError(f'Archive member set mismatch: {bundle["path"]}')
        for name, record in expected.items():
            info = archive.getinfo(name)
            if info.is_dir() or stat.S_ISLNK(info.external_attr >> 16):
                raise ValueError(f'Archive member is not a regular file: {name}')
            content = archive.read(name)
            if len(content) != record['bytes'] or sha(content) != record['sha256']:
                raise ValueError(f'Member checksum mismatch: {name}')
            yield name, content


def restore_bundles(bundles: list[dict], *, verify_only: bool = False) -> int:
    """Preflight every selected bundle and destination before restoring anything."""
    if len({bundle['path'] for bundle in bundles}) != len(bundles):
        raise ValueError('Duplicate logical archive paths')
    seen = set()
    for bundle in bundles:
        for name, content in checked_members(bundle):
            if name in seen:
                raise ValueError(f'Member occurs in multiple bundles: {name}')
            seen.add(name)
            target = safe_path(name, ('data', 'results'))
            if not verify_only and target.exists() and (not target.is_file() or target.read_bytes() != content):
                raise ValueError(f'Existing file differs; move it aside before restoring: {target}')
    if not verify_only:
        for bundle in bundles:
            for name, content in checked_members(bundle):
                target = safe_path(name, ('data', 'results'))
                if target.exists():
                    if not target.is_file() or target.read_bytes() != content:
                        raise ValueError(f'Existing file differs; move it aside before restoring: {target}')
                    continue
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                # Exclusive creation preserves local files even if another
                # process creates one after the preflight check.
                with target.open('xb') as stream:
                    stream.write(content)
    return len(seen)


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--version', help='Restore only this selected release (all by default)')
    parser.add_argument('--verify-only', action='store_true', help='Check archives and member hashes without writing')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / 'archives/manifest.json').read_text())
    if manifest.get('schema_version', 1) not in (1, 2, 3):
        raise ValueError('Unsupported archive manifest schema')
    if manifest.get('schema_version') == 2 and manifest.get('physical_part_size_bytes') != PART_BYTES:
        raise ValueError('Unexpected multipart archive size policy')
    bundles = [b for b in manifest['bundles'] if not args.version or b.get('version') == args.version]
    if not bundles:
        parser.error('Version is not in the preserved dataset')
    files = restore_bundles(bundles, verify_only=args.verify_only)
    for bundle in bundles:
        print(f'{bundle["path"]}: verified {bundle["member_count"]} files', flush=True)
    print(f'Verified {files} files in {len(bundles)} archives' + ('; no files written' if args.verify_only else '; working hierarchy restored'))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
tests/test_archives.py · 14,207 bytes

"""Round trips and failure boundaries for encoded and legacy evidence archives."""
import base64
from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import io
from pathlib import Path
import random
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
import zipfile


REPOSITORY = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def script(name):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, REPOSITORY / 'scripts' / (name + '.py'))
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


packager = script('package_data')
unpacker = script('unpack_data')


class ArchiveTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temporary.name)
        self.patches = [patch.object(module, 'ROOT', self.root) for module in (packager, unpacker)]
        for context in self.patches:
            context.start()
            self.addCleanup(context.stop)
        self.contents = {
            'data/raw/demo/a.bin': random.Random(484).randbytes(packager.PART_BYTES * 2 + 137),
            'results/versions/demo/b.txt': b'Complete evidence; preserved newlines.\r\n',
        }
        for name, content in self.contents.items():
            target = self.root / name
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_bytes(content)

    def bundle(self, multipart=True):
        return packager.pack('versions/guava-10.0.zip', [self.root / n for n in self.contents], multipart=multipart)

    def clear_sources(self):
        for name in ('data', 'results'):
            if (self.root / name).exists():
                shutil.rmtree(self.root / name)

    def assert_no_restoration(self):
        self.assertFalse((self.root / 'data').exists())
        self.assertFalse((self.root / 'results').exists())

    def test_deterministic_multipart_round_trip_and_standard_zip_bytes(self):
        bundle = self.bundle()
        self.assertEqual(bundle, self.bundle())
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(bundle['parts']), 3)
        self.assertTrue(all(p['bytes'] == 512 * 1024 for p in bundle['parts'][:-1]))
        self.assertFalse((self.root / bundle['path']).exists())
        content = unpacker.logical_zip_bytes(bundle)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(content)) as archive:
            self.assertEqual({n: archive.read(n) for n in archive.namelist()}, self.contents)
        self.clear_sources()
        self.assertEqual(unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle]), 2)

        self.assertEqual({n: (self.root / n).read_bytes() for n in self.contents}, self.contents)
        self.assertEqual(unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle]), 2)

    def test_default_base64_round_trip_preserves_exact_standard_zip(self):
        bundle = self.bundle(multipart=None)
        self.assertEqual(bundle['storage'], 'base64')
        self.assertEqual(bundle, self.bundle(multipart=None))
        encoded = bundle['encoded_file']
        stored = (self.root / encoded['path']).read_bytes()
        self.assertEqual(encoded['path'], bundle['path'] + '.b64')
        self.assertEqual(encoded['text_encoding'], 'ascii')
        self.assertEqual(stored.decode('ascii').encode('utf-8'), stored)
        self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(stored).hexdigest(), encoded['sha256'])
        self.assertEqual(encoded['bytes'], 4 * ((bundle['bytes'] + 2) // 3))
        legacy = self.bundle(multipart=True)
        content = unpacker.logical_zip_bytes(bundle)
        self.assertEqual(base64.b64decode(stored, validate=True), content)
        self.assertEqual(content, unpacker.logical_zip_bytes(legacy))
        self.assertFalse((self.root / bundle['path']).exists())
        self.clear_sources()
        self.assertEqual(unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle]), 2)
        self.assertEqual({n: (self.root / n).read_bytes() for n in self.contents}, self.contents)
        self.assertEqual(unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle]), 2)

    def test_missing_encoded_file_does_not_restore_any_member(self):
        bundle = self.bundle(multipart=None)
        (self.root / bundle['encoded_file']['path']).unlink()
        self.clear_sources()
        with self.assertRaises(FileNotFoundError):
            unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle])
        self.assert_no_restoration()

    def test_encoded_checksum_detects_corrupted_text_before_restoration(self):
        bundle = self.bundle(multipart=None)
        physical = self.root / bundle['encoded_file']['path']
        encoded = bytearray(physical.read_bytes())
        encoded[0] = ord('A') if encoded[0] != ord('A') else ord('B')
        physical.write_bytes(encoded)
        self.clear_sources()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'Encoded ZIP checksum'):
            unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle])
        self.assert_no_restoration()

    def test_decoded_zip_hash_is_checked_when_encoded_hash_matches(self):
        bundle = self.bundle(multipart=None)
        physical = self.root / bundle['encoded_file']['path']
        content = bytearray(unpacker.logical_zip_bytes(bundle))
        content[-1] ^= 1
        encoded = base64.b64encode(content)
        physical.write_bytes(encoded)
        bundle['encoded_file']['sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()
        self.clear_sources()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'Logical ZIP checksum'):
            unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle])
        self.assert_no_restoration()

    def test_strict_base64_rejects_whitespace_and_noncanonical_padding(self):
        original = packager.pack('versions/guava-empty.zip', [])
        physical = self.root / original['encoded_file']['path']
        original_encoded = physical.read_bytes()
        self.assertTrue(original_encoded.endswith(b'=='))
        self.clear_sources()
        alphabet = b'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'
        for mutation, message in [('whitespace', 'strict base64'), ('padding_bits', 'not canonical')]:
            with self.subTest(mutation=mutation):
                bundle = deepcopy(original)
                encoded = bytearray(original_encoded)
                if mutation == 'whitespace':
                    encoded[0] = ord(' ')
                else:
                    encoded[-3] = alphabet[alphabet.index(encoded[-3]) + 1]
                physical.write_bytes(encoded)
                bundle['encoded_file']['sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, message):
                    unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle])
                self.assert_no_restoration()

    def test_unsafe_encoded_file_paths_and_symlinks_are_rejected(self):

        self.clear_sources()
        for name in ('archives/../outside.b64', '/tmp/outside.b64', 'archives/wrong.zip.b64'):

                bundle['encoded_file']['path'] = name
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    unpacker.restore_bundles([bundle])
                self.assert_no_restoration()

        elsewhere = self.root / 'elsewhere.b64'
        physical.replace(elsewhere)
        physical.symlink_to(elsewhere)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'Symlink'):
            unpacker.restore_bundles([original])
        self.assert_no_restoration()

    def test_base64_cleanup_preserves_new_storage_and_removes_old_parts(self):

        new = self.bundle(multipart=None)
        self.assertEqual(old['sha256'], new['sha256'])

        packager.remove_superseded_storage([new])

        self.assertTrue(all(not (self.root / p['path']).exists() for p in old['parts']))
        self.assertEqual(unpacker.restore_bundles([new], verify_only=True), 2)

    def test_legacy_whole_zip_and_unchanged_whole_file(self):
        bundle = self.bundle(multipart=False)
        original = (self.root / bundle['path']).stat().st_mtime_ns

"""Deterministic structural KMeans and an adapter to the original York ACDC.

KMeans uses fixed union-class coordinates, outgoing and incoming neighbours,
identity only in features, and row L2 normalization. The metric graph always
remains a directed, binary graph without self-loops or external nodes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter, defaultdict
from collections.abc import Iterable
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import time
import warnings

import numpy as np
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from sklearn.preprocessing import normalize
from threadpoolctl import threadpool_limits

from .metrics import validate_graph, validate_partition
from .toolchain import ROOT, java_env, java_path


def choose_k(n_full: int, n_remaining: int | None = None, *, pilot_n: int, pilot_k: int) -> int:
    """Transfer one pilot's empirically selected granularity to every version.

    Exact integer ceil(n_full * pilot_k / pilot_n), reused for full/filtered
    conditions. This is an explicit extrapolation, not a per-version optimum.
    """
    if n_full < 0 or (n_remaining is not None and not 0 <= n_remaining <= n_full):
        raise ValueError("Invalid full/remaining node counts")
    if not isinstance(pilot_n, int) or not isinstance(pilot_k, int) or not 1 <= pilot_k <= pilot_n:
        raise ValueError("Pilot calibration must have 1 <= pilot_k <= pilot_n")
    remaining = n_full if n_remaining is None else n_remaining
    if remaining == 0:
        return 0
    return min(remaining, max(1, (n_full * pilot_k + pilot_n - 1) // pilot_n))


def build_features(nodes: Iterable[str], edges: Iterable[tuple[str, str]], universe: Iterable[str]) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], csr_matrix]:
    """Sparse X=[A+I,(A+I).T], embedded into the supplied union coordinates.

    The row order is sorted class names. Column order is the exact supplied
    universe, outgoing first and incoming second. Absent/removed classes retain
    their coordinates but have zero columns; filtered graphs must pass only
    retained nodes and retained-endpoint edges. No package labels are features.
    """
    nodes, edges = validate_graph(nodes, edges)
    universe = tuple(universe)
    if any(not isinstance(node, str) or not node for node in universe) or len(set(universe)) != len(universe):
        raise ValueError("Feature universe must contain unique class identifiers")
    columns = {node: index for index, node in enumerate(universe)}
    if not set(nodes) <= columns.keys():
        raise ValueError("Feature universe omits current graph classes")
    rows = {node: index for index, node in enumerate(nodes)}
    width = len(universe)
    row_ids, column_ids = [], []
    for node in nodes:
        row_ids.extend((rows[node], rows[node]))
        column_ids.extend((columns[node], width + columns[node]))
    for source, target in edges:
        row_ids.extend((rows[source], rows[target]))
        column_ids.extend((columns[target], width + columns[source]))
    matrix = csr_matrix((np.ones(len(row_ids), dtype=np.float64), (row_ids, column_ids)), shape=(len(nodes), 2 * width))
    matrix.sort_indices()
    if nodes:
        matrix = normalize(matrix, norm="l2", axis=1, copy=False)
    return nodes, matrix


def _canonical_assignment(nodes: Iterable[str], labels: Iterable[object], prefix: str) -> dict[str, str]:
    groups: dict[object, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    for node, label in zip(nodes, labels, strict=True):
        groups[label].append(node)
    ordered = sorted(groups.values(), key=lambda members: min(members))
    return {node: f"{prefix}_{index:04d}" for index, members in enumerate(ordered) for node in sorted(members)}


def cluster_kmeans(nodes: Iterable[str], edges: Iterable[tuple[str, str]], universe: Iterable[str], k: int, random_state: int = 484, n_init: int = 10) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict]:
    """Run the frozen KMeans configuration once and retain warnings/diagnostics."""
    started = time.perf_counter()
    universe = tuple(universe)
    ordered, matrix = build_features(nodes, edges, universe)
    if not isinstance(k, int) or k < 0 or (ordered and k < 1):
        raise ValueError("k must be positive for a nonempty graph")
    requested = k
    effective = min(k, len(ordered))
    if n_init < 1:
        raise ValueError("n_init must be positive")
    caught = []
    if ordered:
        model = KMeans(n_clusters=effective, init="k-means++", n_init=n_init, max_iter=300, tol=1e-4, random_state=random_state, algorithm="lloyd")
        with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as caught, threadpool_limits(limits=1):
            warnings.simplefilter("always")
            labels = model.fit_predict(matrix)
        assignment = _canonical_assignment(ordered, labels, "kmeans")
        inertia, iterations = float(model.inertia_), int(model.n_iter_)
    else:
        assignment, inertia, iterations = {}, 0.0, 0
    validate_partition(ordered, assignment)
    sizes = Counter(assignment.values())
    metadata = {
        "method": "KMeans",
        "requested_k": requested,
        "clamped_k": effective,
        "actual_k": len(sizes),
        "random_state": random_state,
        "n_init": n_init,
        "max_iter": 300,
        "tol": 1e-4,
        "algorithm": "lloyd",
        "features": "[A+I,(A+I).T] in fixed union coordinates; row L2 normalized",
        "feature_rows": matrix.shape[0],
        "feature_columns": matrix.shape[1],
        "feature_nonzeros": matrix.nnz,
        "feature_universe_sha256": hashlib.sha256(("\n".join(universe) + "\n").encode()).hexdigest(),
        "inertia": inertia,
        "iterations": iterations,
        "warnings": [str(item.message) for item in caught],
        "min_cluster_size": min(sizes.values(), default=0),
        "max_cluster_size": max(sizes.values(), default=0),
        "singleton_clusters": sum(size == 1 for size in sizes.values()),
        "elapsed_seconds": time.perf_counter() - started,
    }
    return dict(sorted(assignment.items())), metadata


def parse_acdc_rsf(text: str, nodes: Iterable[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Flatten a valid containment forest to the immediate parent of each class.

    ACDC's default output is already fine-grained/flat. Accepting a hierarchy
    here is explicit: groups are direct sibling classes, not top-level groups.
    Missing classes are returned as missing; the caller distinguishes genuine
    isolates from a tool failure before filling any memberships.
    """
    expected = set(nodes)
    parents: dict[str, str] = {}
    for number, line in enumerate(text.splitlines(), 1):
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        fields = line.split()
        if len(fields) != 3 or fields[0] != "contain":
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid ACDC containment at line {number}: {line!r}")
        _, parent, child = fields
        if child in parents:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate ACDC membership for {child}")
        if parent == child or parent in expected:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid ACDC container at line {number}")
        parents[child] = parent
    containers = set(parents.values())
    unknown_leaves = set(parents) - containers - expected
    if unknown_leaves:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown classes in ACDC output: {sorted(unknown_leaves)!r}")
    for start in parents:
        seen = set()
        current = start
        while current in parents:
            if current in seen:
                raise ValueError("Cyclic ACDC containment hierarchy")
            seen.add(current)
            current = parents[current]
    return {node: parents[node] for node in sorted(expected) if node in parents}


def run_acdc(nodes: Iterable[str], edges: Iterable[tuple[str, str]], directory: str | Path, java: str | None = None, max_size: int = 20, patterns: str = "so", timeout: float = 300) -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict]:
    """Execute original ACDC; preserve true dependency-free nodes as singletons.

    Input contains only real call edges. Isolates cannot appear in that format,
    so they are explicitly restored outside the unmodified algorithm. Every
    non-isolated class must appear in ACDC output or this function fails.
    max_size bounds SubGraph construction, not the later orphan container.

    Original ACDC assumes file names: Node.getBaseName removes the last dotted
    suffix to name a dominator cluster. Bare Java qualified names would therefore
    collapse different classes in one package to identical cluster names. Each
    class is reversibly encoded as CLASS.java only for the external tool; its
    output is decoded before evaluation. The original Java source is unchanged.
    """
    started = time.perf_counter()
    nodes, edges = validate_graph(nodes, edges)
    if not isinstance(max_size, int) or max_size < 1:
        raise ValueError("ACDC max_size must be a positive integer")
    if not patterns or any(letter not in "bso" for letter in patterns) or len(set(patterns)) != len(patterns):
        raise ValueError("ACDC patterns must be a nonrepeated lowercase subset of bso")
    if any(any(character.isspace() for character in node) for node in nodes):
        raise ValueError("RSF class identifiers cannot contain whitespace")
    directory = Path(directory).resolve()
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    input_path = directory / "input.rsf"
    output_path = directory / "acdc_raw.rsf"
    encoded = {node: node + ".java" for node in nodes}
    decoded = {value: key for key, value in encoded.items()}
    with (directory / "node_id_map.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=["class", "acdc_node"])
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows({"class": node, "acdc_node": encoded[node]} for node in nodes)
    input_path.write_text("".join(f"call {encoded[source]} {encoded[target]}\n" for source, target in edges), encoding="utf-8")
    # Never accept an output left by a prior invocation after an exit(0) error.
    if output_path.exists():
        output_path.unlink()
    incident = {node for edge in edges for node in edge}
    isolates = sorted(set(nodes) - incident)
    command, stdout, stderr, returncode = [], "", "", 0
    raw = {}
    if edges:
        executable = java_path(java)
        classpath = ROOT / "build/acdc"
        if not (classpath / "acdc/ACDC.class").exists():
            raise RuntimeError("ACDC is not compiled; run the Java build step")
        command = [executable, "-Djava.awt.headless=true", "-cp", str(classpath), "acdc.ACDC", str(input_path), str(output_path), "+" + patterns, f"-l{max_size}", "-d1"]
        completed = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, env=java_env(executable), timeout=timeout)
        stdout, stderr, returncode = completed.stdout, completed.stderr, completed.returncode
        (directory / "acdc_stdout.log").write_text(stdout, encoding="utf-8")
        (directory / "acdc_stderr.log").write_text(stderr, encoding="utf-8")
        if returncode or not output_path.exists():
            raise RuntimeError(f"ACDC failed (exit {returncode}); inspect logs in {directory}")
        tool_assignment = parse_acdc_rsf(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), encoded.values())
        raw = {decoded[node]: container for node, container in tool_assignment.items()}
        if set(raw) != incident:
            raise ValueError(f"ACDC coverage mismatch: missing incident nodes {sorted(incident - raw.keys())!r}; unexpected {sorted(raw.keys() - incident)!r}")
    else:
        output_path.write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
        (directory / "acdc_stdout.log").write_text("No dependency edges; all manifest nodes preserved as singleton clusters.\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (directory / "acdc_stderr.log").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    # Tagged label tuples prevent a real container name colliding with an isolate.
    labels = [("acdc", raw[node]) if node in raw else ("isolate", node) for node in nodes]
    assignment = _canonical_assignment(nodes, labels, "acdc")
    validate_partition(nodes, assignment)
    sizes = Counter(assignment.values())
    metadata = {
        "method": "ACDC (original York implementation)",
        "patterns": patterns,
        "subgraph_max_size": max_size,
        "output_mode": "flat fine-grained (default DownInducer)",
        "node_id_encoding": "append_.java_suffix_to_preserve_unique_ACDC_file_basenames",
        "command": command,
        "returncode": returncode,
        "isolates_restored_as_singletons": isolates,
        "original_containers": raw,
        "actual_k": len(sizes),
        "min_cluster_size": min(sizes.values(), default=0),
        "max_cluster_size": max(sizes.values(), default=0),
        "singleton_clusters": sum(size == 1 for size in sizes.values()),
        "elapsed_seconds": time.perf_counter() - started,
    }
    (directory / "acdc_execution.json").write_text(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return dict(sorted(assignment.items())), metadata
src/evolution/k_selection.py · 2,520 bytes

SHA-256: 1b1efe5e6e38d3825de284a4412935f3babc0b1f41979e16c599d83f84bcd9f9

"""One-pilot inertia elbow; no final-MQ information enters the selection."""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping


def select_elbow(points: Iterable[Mapping], *, minimum_gap: float = 0.02,
                 fallback_k: int = 30) -> dict:
    """Find the largest positive gap below the normalized endpoint chord.

    With x=(k-k_min)/(k_max-k_min) and y=(I-I_last)/(I_first-I_last),
    gap=1-x-y. Smaller k wins exact ties. A predeclared nearest-candidate
    fallback handles flat/nondecreasing/no-clear-elbow curves. The full raw
    curve and any nonmonotonicity are retained; no smoothing changes evidence.
    """
    curve = sorted([{"k": int(p["k"]), "inertia": float(p["inertia"])} for p in points], key=lambda p: p["k"])
    if not curve or len({p["k"] for p in curve}) != len(curve):
        raise ValueError("Need a nonempty curve with unique candidate k values")
    if any(p["k"] < 1 or not math.isfinite(p["inertia"]) or p["inertia"] < 0 for p in curve):
        raise ValueError("Candidate k/inertia is invalid")
    if not math.isfinite(minimum_gap) or minimum_gap < 0 or fallback_k < 1:
        raise ValueError("Invalid elbow policy")
    k_span = curve[-1]["k"] - curve[0]["k"]
    i_span = curve[0]["inertia"] - curve[-1]["inertia"]
    for point in curve:
        point["normalized_k"] = (point["k"] - curve[0]["k"]) / k_span if k_span else 0.0
        point["normalized_inertia"] = (point["inertia"] - curve[-1]["inertia"]) / i_span if i_span > 0 else None
        point["chord_gap"] = (1.0 - point["normalized_k"] - point["normalized_inertia"]) if k_span and i_span > 0 else 0.0
    interior = curve[1:-1]
    best = max(interior, key=lambda p: (p["chord_gap"], -p["k"])) if interior else None
    clear = bool(best and best["chord_gap"] >= minimum_gap and i_span > 0)
    selected = best if clear else min(curve, key=lambda p: (abs(p["k"] - fallback_k), p["k"]))
    return {
        "selected_k": selected["k"], "clear_elbow": clear,
        "reason": "maximum_normalized_chord_gap" if clear else "predeclared_fallback_no_clear_elbow",
        "maximum_interior_gap": best["chord_gap"] if best else 0.0,
        "minimum_gap": minimum_gap, "fallback_requested_k": fallback_k,
        "nonmonotone_steps": sum(b["inertia"] > a["inertia"] for a, b in zip(curve, curve[1:])),
        "curve": curve,
        "interpretation": "Pilot inertia geometry only; not an optimal or true architectural cluster count; no MQ used in selection",
    }

"""Feature semantics, deterministic recovery, ACDC parsing and isolate integrity."""
from itertools import permutations
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest

import numpy as np

from evolution.clustering import build_features, choose_k, cluster_kmeans, parse_acdc_rsf, run_acdc
from evolution.toolchain import ROOT


class StructuralFeatureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_directed_neighbour_features_have_expected_coordinates(self):
        nodes, features = build_features(["b", "a"], [("a", "b")], ["a", "b", "absent"])
        self.assertEqual(nodes, ("a", "b"))
        expected = np.array([[1, 1, 0, 1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0, 1, 1, 0]], dtype=float) / np.sqrt(3)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(features.toarray(), expected)
        self.assertEqual(features.nnz, 6)

    def test_removed_features_stay_in_schema_but_have_no_evidence(self):
        universe = ["a", "b", "c", "never_present"]
        _, full = build_features(["a", "b", "c"], [("a", "b"), ("b", "c"), ("a", "c")], universe)
        _, filtered = build_features(["a", "c"], [("a", "c")], universe)
        self.assertEqual(full.shape[1], filtered.shape[1])
        self.assertGreater(full[0, 1], 0)
        self.assertEqual(filtered[:, [1, 5]].nnz, 0)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(np.asarray(filtered.multiply(filtered).sum(axis=1)).ravel(), [1, 1])

    def test_isolated_class_has_identity_features_without_graph_edges(self):
        nodes, matrix = build_features(["alone"], [], ["alone", "absent"])
        self.assertEqual(nodes, ("alone",))
        np.testing.assert_allclose(matrix.toarray(), [[1 / np.sqrt(2), 0, 1 / np.sqrt(2), 0]])

    def test_fixed_full_graph_k_is_reused_then_only_clamped(self):
        calibration = {"pilot_n": 400, "pilot_k": 30}
        self.assertEqual(choose_k(800, **calibration), 60)
        self.assertEqual(choose_k(800, 750, **calibration), 60)
        self.assertEqual(choose_k(801, **calibration), 61)
        self.assertEqual(choose_k(800, 5, **calibration), 5)
        self.assertEqual(choose_k(0, **calibration), 0)
        self.assertEqual(choose_k(1, **calibration), 1)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            choose_k(10, 11, **calibration)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            choose_k(10, pilot_n=0, pilot_k=1)

    def test_kmeans_is_repeatable_after_input_reordering(self):
        nodes = list("abcdef")
        edges = list(permutations("abc", 2)) + list(permutations("def", 2))
        first, meta = cluster_kmeans(nodes, edges, nodes, 2)
        second, second_meta = cluster_kmeans(reversed(nodes), reversed(edges), nodes, 2)
        self.assertEqual(first, second)
        self.assertEqual(len({first[n] for n in "abc"}), 1)
        self.assertEqual(len({first[n] for n in "def"}), 1)
        self.assertNotEqual(first["a"], first["d"])
        self.assertEqual(meta["feature_universe_sha256"], second_meta["feature_universe_sha256"])

    def test_identical_rows_report_fewer_clusters_without_fake_splitting(self):
        nodes = list("abc")
        assignment, metadata = cluster_kmeans(nodes, permutations(nodes, 2), nodes, 2)
        self.assertEqual(metadata["requested_k"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(metadata["actual_k"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(assignment), 3)
        self.assertTrue(metadata["warnings"])

    def test_universe_coverage_and_duplicate_evidence_fail(self):
        for universe in [["a"], ["a", "b", "b"]]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                build_features(["a", "b"], [], universe)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            build_features(["a", "b"], [("a", "b"), ("a", "b")], ["a", "b"])


class AcdcAdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_flat_output_and_hierarchy_have_explicit_leaf_parent_meaning(self):
        flat = parse_acdc_rsf("contain c1 a\ncontain c1 b\ncontain c2 c\n", ["a", "b", "c"])
        nested = parse_acdc_rsf("contain top c1\ncontain top c2\ncontain c1 a\ncontain c1 b\ncontain c2 c\n", ["a", "b", "c"])
        self.assertEqual(flat, nested)
        self.assertEqual(flat, {"a": "c1", "b": "c1", "c": "c2"})

    def test_invalid_containment_is_not_silently_scored(self):
        cases = ["contain c a\ncontain c a\n", "contain c1 a\ncontain c2 a\n",
                 "contain c unknown\n", "call c a\n", "contain c1 c2\ncontain c2 c1\n",
                 "contain a b\n"]
        for text in cases:
            with self.subTest(text=text), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                parse_acdc_rsf(text, ["a", "b"])

    def test_all_isolate_case_needs_no_java_and_preserves_every_node(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            assignments, metadata = run_acdc(["a", "b"], [], directory, java="no-java-needed")
            self.assertEqual(set(assignments), {"a", "b"})
            self.assertEqual(len(set(assignments.values())), 2)
            self.assertEqual(metadata["isolates_restored_as_singletons"], ["a", "b"])
            self.assertEqual(Path(directory, "input.rsf").read_text(), "")

    @unittest.skipUnless((ROOT / "build/acdc/acdc/ACDC.class").exists(), "Build official ACDC before integration test")
    def test_original_java_process_covers_real_edges_and_isolates(self):
        nodes = ["a", "b", "c", "isolated"]
        edges = [("a", "b"), ("b", "a"), ("b", "c")]
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            assignment, metadata = run_acdc(nodes, edges, directory)
            self.assertEqual(set(assignment), set(nodes))
            self.assertEqual(metadata["returncode"], 0)
            self.assertEqual(metadata["isolates_restored_as_singletons"], ["isolated"])
            self.assertNotIn(assignment["isolated"], [assignment[node] for node in "abc"])
            self.assertEqual(set(metadata["original_containers"]), set("abc"))

    @unittest.skipUnless((ROOT / "build/acdc/acdc/ACDC.class").exists(), "Build official ACDC before integration test")
    def test_file_suffix_prevents_same_package_dominator_name_collisions(self):
        # Original ACDC strips the final dotted suffix to name a cluster.
        # With bare Java IDs both disconnected components would be called pkg.ss.
        nodes = ["pkg." + name for name in "abcdef"]
        edges = list(permutations(nodes[:3], 2)) + list(permutations(nodes[3:], 2))
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            assignment, metadata = run_acdc(nodes, edges, directory)
            self.assertEqual(len({assignment[n] for n in nodes[:3]}), 1)
            self.assertEqual(len({assignment[n] for n in nodes[3:]}), 1)
            self.assertNotEqual(assignment[nodes[0]], assignment[nodes[3]])
            self.assertEqual(set(assignment), set(nodes))
            self.assertTrue(all(token.endswith(".java") for line in
                                Path(directory, "input.rsf").read_text().splitlines()
                                for token in line.split()[1:]))
            self.assertEqual(len(set(metadata["original_containers"].values())), 2)

    unittest.main()

"""Known knee, weak curve and invalid-evidence cases for the pilot policy."""
import unittest
from evolution.k_selection import select_elbow


class ElbowTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_known_bend_selected_independently_of_quality_scores(self):
        points = [{"k": k, "inertia": i, "mq": mq} for k, i, mq in
                  [(2, 100, .9), (10, 40, -.8), (20, 35, 1.0), (30, 30, .8)]]
        result = select_elbow(points)
        self.assertEqual(result["selected_k"], 10)
        self.assertTrue(result["clear_elbow"])
        for p in points:
            p["mq"] = 99
        self.assertEqual(select_elbow(points), result)

    def test_linear_curve_uses_predeclared_fallback(self):
        result = select_elbow([{"k": 2, "inertia": 100}, {"k": 16, "inertia": 60}, {"k": 30, "inertia": 20}])
        self.assertFalse(result["clear_elbow"])
        self.assertEqual(result["selected_k"], 30)

    def test_flat_curve_retains_finite_diagnostics(self):
        result = select_elbow([{"k": 2, "inertia": 0}, {"k": 10, "inertia": 0}], fallback_k=30)
        self.assertFalse(result["clear_elbow"])
        self.assertEqual(result["selected_k"], 10)
        self.assertIsNone(result["curve"][0]["normalized_inertia"])

    def test_duplicate_or_nonfinite_evidence_rejected(self):
        for points in ([], [{"k": 2, "inertia": 5}, {"k": 2, "inertia": 1}], [{"k": 2, "inertia": float('nan')}], [{"k": 2, "inertia": -1}]):
            with self.subTest(points=points), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                select_elbow(points)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Optional, explicitly separate parameter exploration for a single release.

This does not alter the frozen study or its configuration. --k lets a reviewer
choose the number of k-means clusters directly without a k sweep.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys

os.environ.setdefault('LOKY_MAX_CPU_COUNT', '1')
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
from evolution.clustering import choose_k, cluster_kmeans, run_acdc
from evolution.graph import read_csv, read_graph, write_json
from evolution.metrics import evaluate_partition, write_metric_tables
from evolution.omnipresent import detect_omnipresent
from evolution.study import save_partition
from evolution.toolchain import build_java

if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--version', default='33.7.2')
    parser.add_argument('--k', type=int, help='Explicit k, shared by full/filtered fits; default frozen size rule')
    parser.add_argument('--acdc-size', type=int, default=20)
    parser.add_argument('--usage-threshold', type=float, default=.85)
    parser.add_argument('--cardinality-threshold', type=float, default=.90)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True, help='Must be a new directory; choose results/rerun/...')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error('Output must be a new directory so recorded results are not overwritten')
    source = ROOT / 'data/graphs' / args.version
    graph = read_graph(source)
    universe = json.loads((ROOT / 'data/graphs/feature_universe.json').read_text())
    noise = detect_omnipresent(graph.nodes, read_csv(source / 'member_dependencies.csv'),
                               usage_threshold=args.usage_threshold, cardinality_threshold=args.cardinality_threshold)
    filtered = graph.without(noise.removed_classes)
    calibration = json.loads((ROOT / 'results/pilot/k_calibration.json').read_text())
    k = args.k if args.k is not None else choose_k(len(graph.nodes), pilot_n=calibration['pilot_n'], pilot_k=calibration['pilot_k'])
    if k < 1 or args.acdc_size < 1:
        parser.error('k and acdc-size must be positive')
    build_java()
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)
    write_json(args.output / 'experiment.json', {**vars(args), 'output': str(args.output), 'k': k,
                'status': 'optional exploration, excluded from frozen30-release results'})
    write_json(args.output / 'ue.json', noise.to_dict(include_dedications=True))
    for condition, method, current in [('A1','ACDC',graph),('A2','ACDC',filtered),('A3','kmeans',graph),('A4','kmeans',filtered)]:
        if method == 'ACDC':
            assignment, metadata = run_acdc(current.nodes, current.edges, args.output / (condition+'_run'), max_size=args.acdc_size)
        else:
            assignment, metadata = cluster_kmeans(current.nodes, current.edges, universe, k, random_state=484, n_init=10)
        save_partition(args.output / (condition+'.csv'), assignment)
        write_json(args.output / (condition+'_run.json'), metadata)
        metrics = evaluate_partition(current.nodes, current.edges, assignment)
        write_json(args.output / (condition+'_metrics.json'), metrics)
        write_metric_tables(metrics, args.output / (condition+'_quality'))
        print(condition, 'clusters', metrics['n_clusters'], 'MQ', metrics['mq'])

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Reproduce Guava extraction, the pilot, and all 120 finalized architectures."""

from evolution.toolchain import build_java

if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--phase", choices=["all", "extract", "pilot", "analyse"], default="all")
    parser.add_argument("--java")
    parser.add_argument("--graph-root", type=Path, default=ROOT / "data/graphs")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=ROOT / "results")
