"""Execute one frozen A1/A2/A3/A4 experiment per pinned Guava release."""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict
from datetime import date
import hashlib
import importlib.metadata
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import sys
import time

from .extraction import extract_release
from .graph import Graph, read_csv, read_graph, sha256, write_csv, write_json
from .omnipresent import detect_omnipresent
from .mojofm import compare_mojofm
from .toolchain import ROOT, build_java, java_env, java_path


def manifest() -> dict:
    result = json.loads((ROOT / "config/versions.json").read_text())
    if result["repository"] != "google/guava":
        raise ValueError("This study must use the user-selected google/guava repository")
    versions = result["versions"]
    if len(versions) != 30 or len({v["version"] for v in versions}) != 30:
        raise ValueError("Expected exactly 30 unique selected releases")
    if len({tuple(v["version"].split(".")[:2]) for v in versions}) != 30:
        raise ValueError("Revised study requires 30 distinct major/minor families")
    if result.get("selection_method") != "major_anchors_calendar_greedy_v3":
        raise ValueError("Expected the frozen major-anchor and calendar-spacing selection rule")
    if {int(v["version"].split(".")[0]) for v in versions} != set(range(10, 34)):
        raise ValueError("The selected history must cover every major release from 10 through 33")
    days = [date.fromisoformat(v["release_date"][:10]).toordinal() for v in versions]
    if any(later - earlier < 60 for earlier, later in zip(days, days[1:])):
        raise ValueError("Selected releases must be ordered at least 60 UTC calendar days apart")
    for row in versions:
        if any(token in row["version"].lower() for token in ("alpha", "beta", "rc", "snapshot", "android")):
            raise ValueError(f"Excluded release variant: {row['version']}")
    return result


def settings() -> dict:
    return json.loads((ROOT / "config/analysis.json").read_text())


def save_partition(path: Path, assignments: dict[str, str]) -> None:
    write_csv(path, [{"class": node, "cluster": str(assignments[node])} for node in sorted(assignments)], ["class", "cluster"])
    path.with_suffix(".rsf").write_text("".join(f"contain {assignments[n]} {n}\n" for n in sorted(assignments)))


def load_partition(path: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    rows = read_csv(path)
    if len(rows) != len({r["class"] for r in rows}):
        raise ValueError(f"Duplicate assignments: {path}")
    return {row["class"]: row["cluster"] for row in rows}


def extract_all(output_root: Path = ROOT / "data/graphs", *, java: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    source = manifest()
    build_java(java)
    summaries = []
    for row in source["versions"]:
        result = extract_release(row, output_root, java=java)
        summaries.append(result)
        print(f"extract {row['version']}: {result['classes']} classes, {result['dependencies']} edges, {result['extraction_seconds']:.2f}s", flush=True)
    universe = sorted({n for row in source["versions"] for n in read_graph(output_root / row["version"]).nodes})
    write_json(output_root / "feature_universe.json", universe)
    write_json(output_root / "extraction_summary.json", summaries)
    return summaries


def _run_acdc(graph: Graph, directory: Path, config: dict, java: str | None, maximum: int | None = None):
    from .clustering import run_acdc
    return run_acdc(graph.nodes, graph.edges, directory,
                    java=java_path(java), max_size=maximum or config["acdc"]["max_subgraph_size"],
                    patterns=config["acdc"]["patterns"].lstrip("+"))


def _run_kmeans(graph: Graph, universe: list[str], n_full: int, config: dict, calibration: dict):
    from .clustering import choose_k, cluster_kmeans
    k = choose_k(n_full, len(graph.nodes), pilot_n=calibration["pilot_n"], pilot_k=calibration["pilot_k"])
    return cluster_kmeans(graph.nodes, graph.edges, universe, k,
                          random_state=config["kmeans"]["random_state"], n_init=config["kmeans"]["n_init"])


def pilot(graph_root: Path = ROOT / "data/graphs", output_root: Path = ROOT / "results/pilot", *, java: str | None = None) -> dict:
    from .metrics import evaluate_partition
    from .clustering import cluster_kmeans
    from .k_selection import select_elbow
    source, config = manifest(), settings()
    row = source["versions"][len(source["versions"]) // 2]
    graph = read_graph(graph_root / row["version"])
    universe = json.loads((graph_root / "feature_universe.json").read_text())
    output_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    trials, acdc_assignments = [], {}
    for maximum in config["pilot"]["acdc_max_subgraph_sizes"]:
        assignment, metadata = _run_acdc(graph, output_root / f"acdc_l{maximum}", config, java, maximum)
        measurement = evaluate_partition(graph.nodes, graph.edges, assignment)
        save_partition(output_root / f"acdc_l{maximum}.csv", assignment)
        trials.append({"algorithm": "ACDC", "max_subgraph_size": maximum, "metrics": measurement, "run": metadata})
        acdc_assignments[maximum] = assignment
    candidates = sorted({min(k, len(graph.nodes)) for k in config["pilot"]["k_candidates"]})
    fits, curve = {}, []
    for k in candidates:
        assignment, metadata = cluster_kmeans(graph.nodes, graph.edges, universe, k,
                          random_state=config["kmeans"]["random_state"], n_init=config["kmeans"]["n_init"])
        fits[k] = (assignment, metadata)
        save_partition(output_root / "k_candidates" / f"k{k}.csv", assignment)
        write_json(output_root / "k_candidates" / f"k{k}_run.json", metadata)
        curve.append({"k": k, "inertia": metadata["inertia"], "actual_k": metadata["actual_k"],
                      "iterations": metadata["iterations"], "elapsed_seconds": metadata["elapsed_seconds"]})
        print(f"pilot inertia k={k}: {metadata['inertia']:.6f}", flush=True)
    elbow = select_elbow(curve, minimum_gap=config["pilot"]["minimum_elbow_gap"],
                         fallback_k=config["pilot"]["fallback_k_if_no_clear_elbow"])
    selected_k = elbow["selected_k"]
    calibration = {"schema_version": 2, "pilot_version": row["version"], "pilot_n": len(graph.nodes), "pilot_k": selected_k,
                   "target_mean_class_count": len(graph.nodes) / selected_k,
                   "rule": config["kmeans"]["k_rule"], "selection": elbow,
                   "config_sha256": sha256(ROOT / "config/analysis.json"),
                   "manifest_sha256": sha256(ROOT / "config/versions.json"),
                   "graph_sha256": sha256(graph_root / row["version"] / "dependencies.csv"),
                   "classes_sha256": sha256(graph_root / row["version"] / "classes.csv"),
                   "feature_universe_sha256": sha256(graph_root / "feature_universe.json"),
                   "quality_scores_used_for_selection": False}
    write_json(output_root / "k_calibration.json", calibration)
    curve_by_k = {item["k"]: item for item in elbow["curve"]}
    write_csv(output_root / "k_inertia_curve.csv", [{**item, **curve_by_k[item["k"]]} for item in curve])
    assignment, metadata = fits[selected_k]
    measurement = evaluate_partition(graph.nodes, graph.edges, assignment)
    save_partition(output_root / "kmeans.csv", assignment)
    trials.append({"algorithm": "kmeans", "metrics": measurement, "run": metadata})
    matched = None
    if config["pilot"].get("matched_acdc_k_diagnostic"):
        default_acdc = acdc_assignments[config["acdc"]["max_subgraph_size"]]
        matched_k = len(set(default_acdc.values()))
        matched_assignment, matched_metadata = fits.get(matched_k, (None, None))
        if matched_assignment is None:
            matched_assignment, matched_metadata = cluster_kmeans(graph.nodes, graph.edges, universe, matched_k,
                       random_state=config["kmeans"]["random_state"], n_init=config["kmeans"]["n_init"])
        save_partition(output_root / "kmeans_matched_acdc_k.csv", matched_assignment)
        matched = {"k": matched_k, "metrics": evaluate_partition(graph.nodes, graph.edges, matched_assignment),
                   "run": matched_metadata, "used_for_k_selection": False,
                   "purpose": "One-pilot granularity diagnostic; not an extra final architecture or selection criterion"}
    result = {"version": row["version"], "selection": "middle release by sample index, chosen before metrics",
              "trials": trials, "k_sweep": "one_pilot_only", "k_calibration": calibration,
              "matched_acdc_k_diagnostic": matched,
              "frozen_configuration_sha256": sha256(ROOT / "config/analysis.json"),
              "decision": "Retain instructor semantics and ACDC +so/-l20. Select one pilot elbow using inertia only; transfer its mean granularity by the frozen integer-ceiling rule to all releases. Report the matched-k diagnostic without optimizing MQ."}
    write_json(output_root / "pilot.json", result)
    print("pilot completed", row["version"], flush=True)
    return result


def analyse_all(graph_root: Path = ROOT / "data/graphs", output_root: Path = ROOT / "results", *, java: str | None = None) -> dict:
    from .metrics import evaluate_partition, write_metric_tables
    source, config = manifest(), settings()
    universe = json.loads((graph_root / "feature_universe.json").read_text())
    calibration_path = output_root / "pilot/k_calibration.json"
    calibration = json.loads(calibration_path.read_text())
    if calibration["config_sha256"] != sha256(ROOT / "config/analysis.json") or calibration["manifest_sha256"] != sha256(ROOT / "config/versions.json"):
        raise ValueError("Pilot calibration does not match the frozen configuration and sample; rerun the pilot")
    metric_rows, size_rows, common_rows, temporal_rows, runtime_rows = [], [], [], [], []
    comparison_rows = []
    previous = None
    for row in source["versions"]:
        version = row["version"]
        comparison_details = []
        directory = output_root / "versions" / version
        directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        graph = read_graph(graph_root / version)
        provenance = json.loads((graph_root / version / "provenance.json").read_text())
        started = time.perf_counter()
        noise = detect_omnipresent(graph.nodes, read_csv(graph_root / version / "member_dependencies.csv"),
                                   usage_threshold=config["omnipresent"]["usage_threshold"],
                                   cardinality_threshold=config["omnipresent"]["cardinality_threshold"])
        ue_seconds = time.perf_counter() - started
        write_json(directory / "ue.json", noise.to_dict(include_dedications=True))
        write_csv(directory / "omnipresent.csv", [s.to_dict() for s in noise.scores])
        filtered = graph.without(noise.removed_classes)
        filtered.rsf(directory / "filtered.rsf")
        write_csv(directory / "retained_classes.csv", [{"class": n} for n in filtered.nodes], ["class"])
        size_rows.append({"index": row["index"], "version": version, "date": row["release_date"],
                          **{k: provenance[k] for k in ("classes", "dependencies", "raw_binary_classes", "merged_binary_classes", "excluded_binary_classes", "java_code_lines", "physical_java_lines", "unique_member_edges", "isolated_classes")},
                          "annotation_classes": provenance["annotation_classes"],
                          "non_annotation_classes": len(graph.nodes) - provenance["annotation_classes"],
                          "annotation_isolates": provenance["annotation_isolated_classes"],
                          "annotation_target_edges": provenance["annotation_target_edges"],
                          "omnipresent_classes": len(noise.removed_classes), "retained_classes": len(filtered.nodes),
                          "retained_dependencies": len(filtered.edges),
                          "edge_retention_fraction": len(filtered.edges) / len(graph.edges) if graph.edges else 1.0})
        architectures = {}
        for condition, method, variant in [("A1", "ACDC", graph), ("A2", "ACDC", filtered), ("A3", "kmeans", graph), ("A4", "kmeans", filtered)]:
            started = time.perf_counter()
            if method == "ACDC":
                assignment, metadata = _run_acdc(variant, directory / f"{condition}_run", config, java)
            else:
                assignment, metadata = _run_kmeans(variant, universe, len(graph.nodes), config, calibration)
            elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
            measurement = evaluate_partition(variant.nodes, variant.edges, assignment)
            save_partition(directory / f"{condition}.csv", assignment)
            write_json(directory / f"{condition}_metrics.json", measurement)
            write_metric_tables(measurement, directory / f"{condition}_quality")
            write_json(directory / f"{condition}_run.json", metadata)
            flat = {key: value for key, value in measurement.items() if value is None or isinstance(value, (int, float, str, bool))}
            metric_rows.append({"index": row["index"], "version": version, "date": row["release_date"], "condition": condition,
                                "algorithm": method, "filtered": condition in ("A2", "A4"), **flat})
            architectures[condition] = assignment
            runtime_rows.append({"version": version, "stage": condition, "seconds": elapsed})
        runtime_rows.extend([{"version": version, "stage": "extraction", "seconds": provenance["extraction_seconds"]},
                             {"version": version, "stage": "UE", "seconds": ue_seconds}])
        def record_comparison(scope, source_label, reference_label, a, b, nodes, previous_version="", n_edges=None):
            projected_a, projected_b = {n: a[n] for n in nodes}, {n: b[n] for n in nodes}
            forward, reverse = compare_mojofm(projected_a, projected_b), compare_mojofm(projected_b, projected_a)
            comparison = {"version": version, "previous_version": previous_version, "scope": scope,
                          "source_condition": source_label, "reference_condition": reference_label,
                          "n_nodes": len(nodes), "n_edges": n_edges,
                          "source_clusters": forward["source_clusters"], "reference_clusters": forward["target_clusters"],
                          "mojo_distance": forward["distance"], "mojo_max_distance": forward["max_distance"], "mojofm": forward["score"],
                          "reverse_mojo_distance": reverse["distance"], "reverse_mojo_max_distance": reverse["max_distance"], "reverse_mojofm": reverse["score"],
                          "node_universe_sha256": hashlib.sha256(("\n".join(sorted(nodes)) + "\n").encode()).hexdigest(),
                          "interpretation": "directional partition agreement; reference is not validated ground truth"}
            comparison_rows.append(comparison)
            comparison_details.append({"comparison": comparison, "forward": forward, "reverse": reverse})
            return comparison
        for full, without in [("A1", "A2"), ("A3", "A4")]:
            projected = {n: architectures[full][n] for n in filtered.nodes}
            baseline = evaluate_partition(filtered.nodes, filtered.edges, projected)
            recovered = evaluate_partition(filtered.nodes, filtered.edges, architectures[without])
            write_json(directory / f"{full}_projected_to_retained_metrics.json", baseline)
            comparison = record_comparison("full_vs_filtered_retained", full, without, projected, architectures[without], filtered.nodes, n_edges=len(filtered.edges))
            common_rows.append({"version": version, "full_condition": full, "filtered_condition": without,
                                "common_classes": len(filtered.nodes), "common_edges": len(filtered.edges),
                                "projected_full_mq": baseline["mq"], "recovered_filtered_mq": recovered["mq"],
                                "mq_change_same_graph": recovered["mq"] - baseline["mq"] if baseline["mq"] is not None else None,
                                "mojofm_full_to_filtered": comparison["mojofm"], "mojofm_filtered_to_full": comparison["reverse_mojofm"],
                                "mojo_distance_full_to_filtered": comparison["mojo_distance"],
                                "mojo_distance_filtered_to_full": comparison["reverse_mojo_distance"]})
        record_comparison("algorithms_full", "A1", "A3", architectures["A1"], architectures["A3"], graph.nodes, n_edges=len(graph.edges))
        record_comparison("algorithms_retained", "A2", "A4", architectures["A2"], architectures["A4"], filtered.nodes, n_edges=len(filtered.edges))
        if previous:
            old_graph, old_architectures, old_version = previous
            common = tuple(sorted(set(old_graph.nodes) & set(graph.nodes)))
            common_set = set(common)
            old_edges = {e for e in old_graph.edges if set(e) <= common_set}
            new_edges = {e for e in graph.edges if set(e) <= common_set}
            union = old_edges | new_edges
            acdc_comparison = record_comparison("temporal_full_common", "A1", "A1", old_architectures["A1"], architectures["A1"], common, previous_version=old_version)
            kmeans_comparison = record_comparison("temporal_full_common", "A3", "A3", old_architectures["A3"], architectures["A3"], common, previous_version=old_version)
            temporal_rows.append({"previous_version": old_version, "version": version,
                                  "common_classes": len(common), "added_classes": len(set(graph.nodes) - set(old_graph.nodes)),
                                  "removed_classes": len(set(old_graph.nodes) - set(graph.nodes)),
                                  "common_node_edge_jaccard": len(old_edges & new_edges) / len(union) if union else 1.0,
                                  "acdc_mojofm_previous_to_current": acdc_comparison["mojofm"],
                                  "acdc_mojofm_current_to_previous": acdc_comparison["reverse_mojofm"],
                                  "kmeans_mojofm_previous_to_current": kmeans_comparison["mojofm"],
                                  "kmeans_mojofm_current_to_previous": kmeans_comparison["reverse_mojofm"]})
        write_json(directory / "partition_comparisons.json", {"version": version, "comparisons": comparison_details})
        previous = (graph, architectures, version)
        print(f"analyse {version}: {len(graph.nodes)} classes; UE removed {len(noise.removed_classes)}; A1-A4 complete", flush=True)
    for filename, rows in [("sizes.csv", size_rows), ("metrics.csv", metric_rows), ("same_graph_comparisons.csv", common_rows),
                           ("evolution.csv", temporal_rows), ("runtimes.csv", runtime_rows), ("partition_comparisons.csv", comparison_rows)]:
        write_csv(output_root / filename, rows)
    run = {"subject": "google/guava", "release_count": len(size_rows), "architecture_count": len(metric_rows),
           "study_revision": config["study_revision"], "comparison_count": len(comparison_rows),
           "k_calibration_sha256": sha256(calibration_path), "pilot_n": calibration["pilot_n"], "pilot_k": calibration["pilot_k"],
           "manifest_sha256": sha256(ROOT / "config/versions.json"), "config_sha256": sha256(ROOT / "config/analysis.json"),
           "python": sys.version, "platform": platform.platform(),
           "packages": {n: importlib.metadata.version(n) for n in ("numpy", "scipy", "scikit-learn", "matplotlib", "reportlab")},
           "feature_universe_size": len(universe), "feature_dimensions": 2 * len(universe),
           "source_sha256": {str(p.relative_to(ROOT)): sha256(p) for p in sorted((ROOT / "src/evolution").glob("*.py"))},
           "java_toolchain": json.loads((ROOT / "build/toolchain.json").read_text()),
           "time_measurement": "perf_counter wall seconds; extraction includes JVM startup and IO; UE excludes member-CSV parsing only when explicitly benchmarked separately"}
    if run["architecture_count"] != 120:
        raise ValueError("Incomplete 30 x 4 study")
    write_json(output_root / "run_manifest.json", run)
    return run

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Score the recorded AI partition only after its blind recovery is complete."""
from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
from evolution.graph import read_csv, read_graph, write_csv, write_json, sha256
from evolution.metrics import evaluate_partition, write_metric_tables
from evolution.mojofm import compare_mojofm
from evolution.study import load_partition


def main() -> None:
    directory = ROOT / 'results/ai'
    record = json.loads((directory / 'experiment.json').read_text())
    config = json.loads((ROOT / 'config/analysis.json').read_text())
    manifest = json.loads((ROOT / 'config/versions.json').read_text())
    version = record['version']
    if version != manifest['versions'][-1]['version']:
        raise ValueError('Recorded AI response does not target the last selected release')
    if sha256(ROOT / record['prompt_file']) != record['prompt_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Recorded prompt changed')
    for item in record['input_files']:
        if sha256(ROOT / item['path']) != item['sha256']:
            raise ValueError(f'AI evidence changed: {item["path"]}')
    for item in record.get('additional_prompt_files', []):
        if sha256(ROOT / item['path']) != item['sha256']:
            raise ValueError(f'Additional prompt changed: {item["path"]}')
    source_provenance = directory / 'source_graph_provenance.json'
    if sha256(source_provenance) != record['input_provenance']['graph_provenance_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Recorded source graph provenance changed')
    for filename in ('classes.csv', 'dependencies.csv'):
        if sha256(directory / 'inputs' / filename) != sha256(ROOT / 'data/graphs' / version / filename):
            raise ValueError(f'Current graph no longer matches the recorded AI input: {filename}')
    graph = read_graph(ROOT / 'data/graphs' / version)
    assignment = load_partition(directory / 'assignment.csv')
    metrics = evaluate_partition(graph.nodes, graph.edges, assignment)
    architecture = json.loads((directory / 'architecture.json').read_text())
    declared = {item['id']: item['class_count'] for item in architecture['modules']}
    if declared != dict(Counter(assignment.values())):
        raise ValueError('AI module declaration counts disagree with actual membership')
    # The archived retained-class manifest is authoritative for comparison scope.
    retained = {r['class'] for r in read_csv(ROOT / 'results/versions' / version / 'retained_classes.csv')}
    filtered = graph.without(set(graph.nodes) - retained)
    projected = {n: assignment[n] for n in filtered.nodes}
    retained_metrics = evaluate_partition(filtered.nodes, filtered.edges, projected)
    write_json(directory / 'metrics.json', metrics)
    write_json(directory / 'retained_metrics.json', retained_metrics)
    write_metric_tables(metrics, directory / 'quality_full')
    write_metric_tables(retained_metrics, directory / 'quality_retained')
    comparisons = []
    details = []
    for condition in ('A1', 'A2', 'A3', 'A4'):
        current = filtered if condition in ('A2', 'A4') else graph
        baseline = load_partition(ROOT / 'results/versions' / version / (condition + '.csv'))
        base_metrics = evaluate_partition(current.nodes, current.edges, baseline)
        ai = projected if current is filtered else assignment
        ai_metrics = retained_metrics if current is filtered else metrics
        forward, reverse = compare_mojofm(ai, baseline), compare_mojofm(baseline, ai)
        comparisons.append({'condition': condition, 'graph': 'retained' if current is filtered else 'full',
                            'n_nodes': len(current.nodes), 'n_edges': len(current.edges),
                            'baseline_mq': base_metrics['mq'], 'ai_mq': ai_metrics['mq'],
                            'mojofm_ai_to_baseline': forward['score'],
                            'mojofm_baseline_to_ai': reverse['score'],
                            'mojo_distance_ai_to_baseline': forward['distance'],
                            'mojo_distance_baseline_to_ai': reverse['distance'],
                            'mojo_max_distance_ai_to_baseline': forward['max_distance'],
                            'mojo_max_distance_baseline_to_ai': reverse['max_distance'],
                            'node_universe_sha256': hashlib.sha256(('\n'.join(sorted(current.nodes)) + '\n').encode()).hexdigest()})
        details.append({'comparison': comparisons[-1], 'ai_to_baseline': forward, 'baseline_to_ai': reverse})
    write_csv(directory / 'comparisons.csv', comparisons)
    write_json(directory / 'partition_comparisons.json', {'version': version, 'comparisons': details})
    outputs = ['initial_hypothesis.md', 'assignment.csv', 'architecture.json', 'response.md', 'activity.json']
    if not all((directory / name).is_file() for name in outputs):
        raise ValueError('Incomplete recorded AI response')
    write_json(directory / 'evaluation.json', {'version': version, 'status': 'passed',
               'study_revision': config['study_revision'],
               'source_experiment_revision': record['study_revision'],
               'evaluation_of_preserved_assignment': True,
               'config_sha256': sha256(ROOT / 'config/analysis.json'),
               'manifest_sha256': sha256(ROOT / 'config/versions.json'),
               'source_graph_provenance_file': str(source_provenance.relative_to(ROOT)),
               'source_graph_provenance_sha256': sha256(source_provenance),
               'current_graph_provenance_sha256': sha256(ROOT / 'data/graphs' / version / 'provenance.json'),
               'classes_assigned_exactly_once': len(assignment), 'modules': len(declared),
               'external_or_invented_classes': 0, 'input_hashes_verified': True,
               'output_sha256': {name: sha256(directory / name) for name in outputs},
               'metric_scope': 'Every comparison uses identical class and edge sets; retained AI assignment is a projection, not a new AI recovery.',
               'quality_claim': 'MoJoFM in both directions measures partition agreement, not accuracy. No validated intended-architecture ground truth is available.'})
    print(json.dumps({'version': version, 'modules': len(declared), 'full_mq': metrics['mq'], 'retained_mq': retained_metrics['mq'], 'comparisons': comparisons}, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
scripts/prepare_ai_inputs.py · 8,730 bytes

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Prepare reproducible evidence for one fresh, blinded AI recovery session.

This only prepares evidence. An actual independent AI session must produce the
architecture; this script never fabricates a model response or partition.
"""
from collections import Counter
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import sys
from zipfile import ZipFile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
from evolution.graph import read_csv, sha256, write_csv, write_json


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, default=ROOT / 'results/ai')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    directory = args.output.resolve()
    if (directory / 'assignment.csv').exists():
        raise ValueError('A completed response already exists; use a new --output directory')
    row = json.loads((ROOT / 'config/versions.json').read_text())['versions'][-1]
    version = row['version']
    graph = ROOT / 'data/graphs' / version
    classes, edges = read_csv(graph / 'classes.csv'), read_csv(graph / 'dependencies.csv')
    inputs = directory / 'inputs'
    inputs.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name in ('classes.csv', 'dependencies.csv'):
        shutil.copyfile(graph / name, inputs / name)
    packages = {r['class']: r['package'] for r in classes}
    write_json(inputs / 'package_counts.json', dict(sorted(Counter(packages.values()).items())))
    counts = Counter((packages[r['source']], packages[r['target']]) for r in edges)
    write_csv(inputs / 'package_dependencies.csv',
              [{'source_package': a, 'target_package': b, 'dependencies': count}
               for (a, b), count in sorted(counts.items())])
    source = ROOT / row['sources_path']
    if sha256(source) != row['sources_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('Source JAR does not match pinned release')
    with ZipFile(source) as archive:
        names = set(archive.namelist())
        documents = []
        for package in sorted(set(packages.values())):
            name = package.replace('.', '/') + '/package-info.java'
            if name in names:
                documents.append(f'===== {name} =====\n' + archive.read(name).decode('utf-8') + '\n')
    (inputs / 'package_documentation.txt').write_text('\n'.join(documents), encoding='utf-8')
    relative = directory.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
    prompt = f'''Recover a maintainable conceptual module architecture for the latest selected Guava JRE release, {version}, from the supplied evidence. This is an actual recorded AI architecture-recovery experiment for EPL484 Software Evolution. You are a fresh AI reviewer and must not inspect any ACDC/KMeans partitions, UE decisions, metric outcomes, reports, or other experiment outputs.

Use only the following files under {relative}/inputs (all from the same pinned release): classes.csv ({len(classes)} canonical outer types), dependencies.csv ({len(edges)} binary directed internal class dependencies), package_counts.json, package_dependencies.csv, and package_documentation.txt (unmodified package documentation from the matching source JAR). The primary dependencies are the instructor DependencyExtractor relationships. External libraries have already been excluded and inner/anonymous classes merged. The inventory includes all owned types, including isolated classes and annotation declarations; annotation applications are not edges in this graph. Both com.google.common and Guava-owned com.google.thirdparty.publicsuffix are in scope. A dependency points from a class using a type/member to the class being used. Package declarations and documentation are evidence, not a validated intended architecture. Do not browse external sources or inspect other project files. You may use Python to read and summarize only these inputs and produce the requested outputs.

First inspect classes.csv and package_counts.json only. Record a concise initial architecture hypothesis in {relative}/initial_hypothesis.md before reading dependency/documentation inputs. This hypothesis is qualitative, not a second finalized architecture. Then read the remaining evidence and refine the hypothesis into ONE complete final class partition. Select a practical number of named responsibility modules; do not target a numeric clustering metric. Classes in one package may be split if responsibility boundaries and dependency evidence justify it. Preserve isolated types. Give every supplied class exactly one module and invent no classes. Do not place a class in multiple modules, including utilities.

scripts/audit_change_points.py · 14,856 bytes

SHA-256: a363af13ad4b62a7cf88ef50eff5e023633e63b955629275ff961b1f42124bfa

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Reproduce the graph/source evidence behind the reported Guava change points.

Read-only with respect to finalized inputs: writes only results/change_points.json.
In a packaged checkout, first run python scripts/unpack_data.py.
No production extraction, clustering, detector or metric function is imported.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter
import csv
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
from zipfile import ZipFile


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def csv_rows(path):
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def sha256(path):
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        return hashlib.file_digest(handle, "sha256").hexdigest()


def require(condition, description):
    if not condition:
        raise ValueError(description)


def snapshot(root, release):
    directory = root / "data/graphs" / release["version"]
    if not (directory / "classes.csv").is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError("Expanded inputs are missing; run python scripts/unpack_data.py first")
    class_records = csv_rows(directory / "classes.csv")
    classes = {r["class"]: r for r in class_records}
    edge_records = csv_rows(directory / "dependencies.csv")
    edge_rows = {(r["source"], r["target"]): i for i, r in enumerate(edge_records, 2)}
    edges = set(edge_rows)
    require(len(classes) == len(class_records), "Duplicate class identity")
    require(len(edges) == len(edge_records), "Duplicate directed binary edge")
    require(all(a in classes and b in classes and a != b for a, b in edges), "Invalid graph scope or loop")
    provenance = json.loads((directory / "provenance.json").read_text())
    hashes = {name: sha256(directory / name) for name in
              ("classes.csv", "dependencies.csv", "dependencies_typed.csv", "provenance.json")}
    require(hashes["classes.csv"] == provenance["classes_sha256"], "Class checksum differs from production provenance")
    require(hashes["dependencies.csv"] == provenance["graph_sha256"], "Edge checksum differs from production provenance")
    require(provenance["binary_sha256"] == release["jar_sha256"], "Wrong binary provenance")
    require(provenance["sources_sha256"] == release["sources_sha256"], "Wrong source provenance")
    return {
        "classes": classes, "edges": edges, "edge_rows": edge_rows,
        "typed_counts": Counter(r["relation"] for r in csv_rows(directory / "dependencies_typed.csv")),
        "provenance": {
            "version": release["version"], "tag": release["tag"], "commit_sha": release["commit_sha"],
            "graph_directory": str(directory.relative_to(root)),
            "production_file_sha256": hashes,
            "binary_artifact": {"path": release["jar_path"], "manifest_sha256": release["jar_sha256"]},
            "sources_artifact": {"path": release["sources_path"], "manifest_sha256": release["sources_sha256"]},
            "extractor_sha256": provenance["extractor_sha256"],
        },
    }


def difference(before_version, after_version, snapshots):
    before, after = snapshots[before_version], snapshots[after_version]
    old_classes, new_classes = before["classes"], after["classes"]
    old_nodes, new_nodes = set(old_classes), set(new_classes)
    old_edges, new_edges = before["edges"], after["edges"]
    added_nodes, removed_nodes = new_nodes - old_nodes, old_nodes - new_nodes
    added_edges, removed_edges = new_edges - old_edges, old_edges - new_edges
    added_annotations = {n for n in added_nodes if new_classes[n]["kind"] == "annotation"}
    removed_annotations = {n for n in removed_nodes if old_classes[n]["kind"] == "annotation"}
    added_to_annotations = {e for e in added_edges if new_classes[e[1]]["kind"] == "annotation"}
    removed_to_removed_annotations = {e for e in removed_edges if e[1] in removed_annotations}
    common = old_nodes & new_nodes
    common_old = {e for e in old_edges if e[0] in common and e[1] in common}
    common_new = {e for e in new_edges if e[0] in common and e[1] in common}
    union = common_old | common_new
    top = lambda pairs: [{"class": n, "edges": count} for n, count in
                         sorted(Counter(b for a, b in pairs).items(), key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]))]
    return {
        "before_version": before_version, "after_version": after_version,
        "counts": {
            "classes_before": len(old_nodes), "classes_after": len(new_nodes),
            "added_classes": len(added_nodes), "removed_classes": len(removed_nodes),
            "added_annotation_classes": len(added_annotations), "removed_annotation_classes": len(removed_annotations),
            "edges_before": len(old_edges), "edges_after": len(new_edges),
            "added_edges": len(added_edges), "removed_edges": len(removed_edges),
            "net_edge_change": len(new_edges) - len(old_edges),
            "edge_change_percent": 100 * (len(new_edges) / len(old_edges) - 1) if old_edges else None,
            "class_change_percent": 100 * (len(new_nodes) / len(old_nodes) - 1) if old_nodes else None,
            "annotation_classes_before": sum(r['kind'] == 'annotation' for r in old_classes.values()),
            "annotation_classes_after": sum(r['kind'] == 'annotation' for r in new_classes.values()),
            "non_annotation_classes_before": sum(r['kind'] != 'annotation' for r in old_classes.values()),
            "non_annotation_classes_after": sum(r['kind'] != 'annotation' for r in new_classes.values()),
            "added_edges_to_annotation_types": len(added_to_annotations),
            "removed_edges_to_removed_annotation_types": len(removed_to_removed_annotations),
            "common_classes": len(common),
            "common_node_edge_jaccard": len(common_old & common_new) / len(union) if union else 1.0,
        },
        "added_classes": [new_classes[n] for n in sorted(added_nodes)],
        "removed_classes": [old_classes[n] for n in sorted(removed_nodes)],
        "added_annotation_classes": sorted(added_annotations),
        "removed_annotation_classes": sorted(removed_annotations),
        "added_classes_by_package": dict(sorted(Counter(new_classes[n]['package'] for n in added_nodes).items())),
        "removed_classes_by_package": dict(sorted(Counter(old_classes[n]['package'] for n in removed_nodes).items())),
        "typed_edge_counts_before": dict(sorted(before["typed_counts"].items())),
        "typed_edge_counts_after": dict(sorted(after["typed_counts"].items())),
        "added_edges_by_target": top(added_edges), "removed_edges_by_target": top(removed_edges),
        "added_edges": [{"source": a, "target": b,
                         "after_dependencies_csv_row_including_header": after["edge_rows"][a, b],
                         "target_is_annotation": new_classes[b]["kind"] == "annotation"}
                        for a, b in sorted(added_edges)],
        "removed_edges": [{"source": a, "target": b,
                           "before_dependencies_csv_row_including_header": before["edge_rows"][a, b],
                           "target_is_removed_annotation": b in removed_annotations}
                          for a, b in sorted(removed_edges)],
    }


def source_markers(root, release):
    archive_path = root / release["sources_path"]
    actual_hash = sha256(archive_path)
    require(actual_hash == release["sources_sha256"], "Pinned source JAR checksum mismatch")
    paths = (
        "com/google/common/collect/Lists.java",
        "com/google/common/collect/package-info.java",
        "com/google/common/collect/ElementTypesAreNonnullByDefault.java",
    )
    needles = ("@ElementTypesAreNonnullByDefault", "@interface ElementTypesAreNonnullByDefault",
               "@NullMarked", "import org.jspecify.annotations.NullMarked;")
    evidence = []
    with ZipFile(archive_path) as archive:
        names = set(archive.namelist())
        for name in paths:
            row = {"source_path_within_jar": name, "present": name in names}
            if name in names:
                content = archive.read(name)
                row.update({
                    "source_file_sha256": hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(content),
                    "matching_lines": [{"line_1_based": i, "text": text, "matched_markers": matches}
                                       for i, text in enumerate(content.decode("utf-8").splitlines(), 1)
                                       if (matches := [needle for needle in needles if needle in text])],
                })
            evidence.append(row)
    return {"version": release["version"], "sources_jar_path": release["sources_path"],
            "sources_jar_sha256": actual_hash, "line_number_basis": "1-based lines of exact UTF-8 source-JAR entry",
            "marker_searches": list(needles), "files": evidence}


def run(root):
    manifest_path = root / "config/versions.json"
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())
    require(manifest["repository"] == "google/guava", "Wrong study subject")
    releases = {r["version"]: r for r in manifest["versions"]}
    selected = [r['version'] for r in manifest['versions']]
    snapshots = {v: snapshot(root, releases[v]) for v in selected}
    pairs = list(zip(selected, selected[1:]))
    changes = [difference(a, b, snapshots) for a, b in pairs]
    size_rows = {r['version']: r for r in csv_rows(root / 'results/sizes.csv')}
    for v in selected:
        require(len(snapshots[v]['classes']) == int(size_rows[v]['classes']), f'Size summary disagrees for {v}')
        require(len(snapshots[v]['edges']) == int(size_rows[v]['dependencies']), f'Edge summary disagrees for {v}')
    unchanged = []
    for a, b in pairs:
        left, right = snapshots[a], snapshots[b]
        same_nodes, same_edges = set(left["classes"]) == set(right["classes"]), left["edges"] == right["edges"]
        if same_nodes and same_edges:
            unchanged.append({"before_version": a, "after_version": b,
                          "same_canonical_class_identities": same_nodes, "same_directed_binary_edges": same_edges,
                          "class_count": len(left["classes"]), "edge_count": len(left["edges"]),
                          "classes_csv_byte_identical": left["provenance"]["production_file_sha256"]["classes.csv"] == right["provenance"]["production_file_sha256"]["classes.csv"],
                          "dependencies_csv_byte_identical": left["provenance"]["production_file_sha256"]["dependencies.csv"] == right["provenance"]["production_file_sha256"]["dependencies.csv"]})
    annotation_change = max(changes, key=lambda r: r['counts']['removed_annotation_classes'])
    sources = [source_markers(root, releases[v]) for v in
               (annotation_change['before_version'], annotation_change['after_version'])]
    # For every observed class addition/removal, preserve the exact source-entry
    # hash. These are observations of selected snapshots, not claims about the
    # first historical introduction/removal of a feature.
    needed = {v: set() for v in selected}
    for change in changes:
        for key, version_key in [('added_classes', 'after_version'), ('removed_classes', 'before_version')]:
            needed[change[version_key]].update(r['source_file'] for r in change[key])
    source_entries = []
    for v in selected:
        path = root / releases[v]['sources_path']
        require(sha256(path) == releases[v]['sources_sha256'], f'Wrong source artifact for {v}')
        with ZipFile(path) as archive:
            names = set(archive.namelist())
            entries = []
            for name in sorted(needed[v]):
                require(name in names, f'Missing owned source {v}: {name}')
                content = archive.read(name)
                entries.append({'source_path_within_jar': name, 'bytes': len(content),
                                'sha256': hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()})
        source_entries.append({'version': v, 'sources_jar_path': releases[v]['sources_path'],
                               'sources_jar_sha256': releases[v]['sources_sha256'], 'files': entries})
    ranked = sorted(changes, key=lambda r: (-abs(r['counts']['edge_change_percent']), r['after_version']))
    return {
        "schema_version": 2, "status": "passed", "audited_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "audit_script_sha256": sha256(Path(__file__)), "versions_manifest_sha256": sha256(manifest_path),
        "method": "Independent set differences over finalized owned-class binary graphs; annotation kinds from class inventory; exact pinned source-JAR entry search",
        "inputs": [snapshots[v]["provenance"] for v in selected], "change_points": changes,
        "transition_count": len(changes),
        "largest_absolute_percentage_edge_changes": [
            {'before_version': r['before_version'], 'after_version': r['after_version'], 'counts': r['counts']}
            for r in ranked[:5]],
        "source_entries_for_observed_class_changes": source_entries,
        "source_evidence": sources, "unchanged_graph_pairs": unchanged,
        "interpretation_limits": [
            "Typed relation counts can overlap within one directed binary class edge and must not be summed as the binary edge count.",
            "Annotation migrations can change the owned-type inventory even when annotation applications are excluded from primary edges. The source markers document metadata changes, not architectural repair.",
            "Largest transitions are ranked for descriptive discussion only after executing the fixed cohort; they do not determine release selection or parameter choices.",
            "Different-length release intervals and bytecode/compiler effects limit causal interpretation; dependency count alone is not semantic complexity.",
            "Equal class/dependency graphs do not imply identical source text or release artifacts.",
            "This audit creates no new architecture and modifies no finalized per-version input/output file.",
        ],
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=ROOT)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    result = run(args.root)
    output = args.output or args.root / "results/change_points.json"
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"status": result["status"], "change_points": len(result["change_points"]),
                      "unchanged_pairs": len(result["unchanged_graph_pairs"]), "output": str(output)}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Distinguish measured graph-model effects from the combined study revision."""
from collections import Counter
import csv
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def rows(path):
    with path.open(newline='') as stream:
        return list(csv.DictReader(stream))


def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def edges(path):
    return {(r['source'], r['target']) for r in rows(path)}


def main():
    baseline = ROOT / 'results/baseline_v1'
    record = json.loads((baseline / 'latest_graph_provenance.json').read_text())
    for name, expected in record['exported_files'].items():
        if digest(baseline / name) != expected:
            raise ValueError('Preserved original graph changed')
    version = record['version']
    directory = ROOT / 'data/graphs' / version
    old_nodes = {r['class'] for r in rows(baseline / 'latest_classes.csv')}
    new_classes = {r['class']: r for r in rows(directory / 'classes.csv')}
    new_nodes = set(new_classes)
    old = edges(baseline / 'latest_dependencies.csv')
    primary = edges(directory / 'dependencies.csv')
    supplemental = edges(directory / 'supplemental_dependencies.csv')
    annotations = {n for n, r in new_classes.items() if r['kind'] == 'annotation'}
    assert old_nodes <= new_nodes
    assert old == {e for e in supplemental if set(e) <= old_nodes}
    assert primary <= supplemental
    added_scope = new_nodes - old_nodes
    assert all(n.startswith('com.google.thirdparty.publicsuffix.') for n in added_scope)
    primary_in_old_scope = {e for e in primary if set(e) <= old_nodes}
    extra = supplemental - primary
    annotation_extra = {e for e in extra if e[1] in annotations}
    incident = {n for e in primary for n in e}
    old_sizes = {r['version']: r for r in rows(baseline / 'sizes.csv')}
    current_sizes = rows(ROOT / 'results/sizes.csv')
    comparisons = []
    for row in current_sizes:
        if row['version'] in old_sizes:
            previous = old_sizes[row['version']]
            comparisons.append({'version': row['version'],
                'original_classes': int(previous['classes']), 'revised_classes': int(row['classes']),
                'original_edges': int(previous['dependencies']), 'revised_edges': int(row['dependencies']),
                'original_ue_removals': int(previous['omnipresent_classes']),
                'revised_ue_removals': int(row['omnipresent_classes'])})
    types = Counter(r['relation'] for r in rows(directory / 'supplemental_dependencies_typed.csv')
                    if (r['source'], r['target']) in extra and r['target'] not in annotations)
    payload = {
        'schema_version': 1, 'status': 'passed', 'original_commit': record['source_commit'],
        'current_manifest_sha256': digest(ROOT / 'config/versions.json'),
        'current_config_sha256': digest(ROOT / 'config/analysis.json'),
        'latest_version': version, 'latest_controlled_graph_decomposition': {
            'original_common_only': {'classes': len(old_nodes), 'edges': len(old)},
            'supplemental_with_corrected_ownership': {'classes': len(new_nodes), 'edges': len(supplemental)},
            'primary_with_corrected_ownership': {'classes': len(new_nodes), 'edges': len(primary)},
            'instructor_on_original_node_scope': {'classes': len(old_nodes), 'edges': len(primary_in_old_scope)},
            'added_owned_classes': sorted(added_scope),
            'extra_supplemental_edges_from_scope': len(supplemental - old),
            'extra_primary_edges_from_scope': len(primary - primary_in_old_scope),
            'same_node_set_supplemental_minus_instructor': len(extra),
            'same_node_set_annotation_application_edges': len(annotation_extra),
            'same_node_set_other_extractor_edges': len(extra - annotation_extra),
            'other_edge_relation_counts_may_overlap': dict(sorted(types.items())),
            'instructor_only_edges': len(primary - supplemental),
            'annotation_nodes': len(annotations),
            'annotation_isolates': len(annotations - incident),
            'primary_annotation_target_edges': sum(b in annotations for a, b in primary),
            'all_primary_isolates': len(new_nodes - incident),
            'original_edges_exactly_equal_supplemental_projection': True},
        'common_release_count': len(comparisons), 'common_release_size_comparisons': comparisons,
        'interpretation': [
            'At the latest release the graph-set audit separates added first-party ownership from extractor semantics, holding the artifact fixed.',
            'The supplementary graph is an audit and member-evidence aid; none of its extra class edges enter the primary clustering graphs.',
            'Whole-study MQ and agreement differences combine sampling, scope, graph semantics and k policy; they cannot be attributed to any one change.',
            'Annotation declarations remain owned nodes, including isolates. Excluding annotation applications does not mean that all annotations lack runtime semantics.',
            'An identical UE removal count alone is not a proof of extractor or detector correctness. Exact set checks, equation checks and compiled fixtures provide stronger evidence.'],
        'input_sha256': {p.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(): digest(p) for p in [
            baseline/'latest_graph_provenance.json', baseline/'latest_classes.csv', baseline/'latest_dependencies.csv',
            directory/'classes.csv', directory/'dependencies.csv', directory/'supplemental_dependencies.csv']}}

scripts/audit_ue_cases.py · 20,569 bytes

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independently recalculate two real UE decisions using exact set definitions."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter
import csv
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
from zipfile import ZipFile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def read_csv(path):
    with path.open(newline="") as stream:
        return list(csv.DictReader(stream))


def rational(value):
    return {"fraction": str(value), "float": float(value)}


def exact_case(class_name, indexed_rows, classes, production, thresholds):
    incoming = [
        (number, row) for number, row in indexed_rows
        if row["target_class"] == class_name
        and row["source_class"] in classes
        and row["target_class"] in classes
        and row["source_class"] != row["target_class"]
    ]
    targets = {row["target_member"] for _, row in incoming}
    callers = {row["source_class"] for _, row in incoming}
    if not targets or not callers:
        raise ValueError(f"Case {class_name} has no incoming member-use evidence")
    source_fanin = {
        member: len({row["source_member"] for _, row in incoming if row["target_member"] == member})
        for member in targets
    }
    # Express the paper's M_AB sets directly.  This audit does not import UE.
    caller_targets = {
        caller: {row["target_member"] for _, row in incoming if row["source_class"] == caller}
        for caller in callers
    }
    dedication = {
        caller: sum(
            (Fraction(1, source_fanin[member] * len(targets)) for member in caller_targets[caller]),
            Fraction(),
        )
        for caller in callers
    }
    strongest = min(dedication, key=lambda caller: (-dedication[caller], caller))
    maximum = dedication[strongest]
    equality = 1 - maximum
    cardinality = 1 - Fraction(1, len(callers))
    removed = equality >= thresholds[0] and cardinality >= thresholds[1]
    score = production[class_name]
    assert score["fanin"] == len(callers)
    assert score["referenced_members"] == len(targets)
    assert score["strongest_caller"] == strongest
    assert score["omnipresent"] == removed
    assert score["usage_equality_passed"] == (equality >= thresholds[0])
    assert score["cardinality_passed"] == (cardinality >= thresholds[1])
    errors = {}
    for name, exact in (("max_dedication", maximum), ("usage_equality", equality), ("cardinality", cardinality)):
        errors[name] = abs(score[name] - float(exact))
        assert math.isclose(score[name], float(exact), rel_tol=0, abs_tol=1e-14)

    def evidence_row(number, row):
        return {"csv_row_number_including_header": number, **row}

    return {
        "class_name": class_name,
        "exact": {
            "fanin": len(callers), "referenced_members": len(targets),
            "max_dedication": rational(maximum), "usage_equality": rational(equality),
            "cardinality": rational(cardinality), "strongest_caller": strongest,
            "omnipresent": removed,
        },
        "production_score": score,
        "verification": {"all_comparisons_passed": True, "absolute_float_errors": errors,
                         "caller_scores_checked_for_maximum": len(callers)},
        "incoming_member_row_count": len(incoming),
        "distinct_incoming_member_pairs": len({(row["source_member"], row["target_member"]) for _, row in incoming}),
        "strongest_caller_terms": [
            {"target_member": member, "source_member_fanin": source_fanin[member],
             "contribution": rational(Fraction(1, source_fanin[member] * len(targets)))}
            for member in sorted(caller_targets[strongest])
        ],
        "strongest_caller_term_fanin_histogram": dict(sorted(Counter(source_fanin[m] for m in caller_targets[strongest]).items())),
        "all_caller_dedications": [
            {"caller": caller, "used_target_members": len(caller_targets[caller]),
             "dedication": rational(dedication[caller])}
            for caller in sorted(callers, key=lambda caller: (-dedication[caller], caller))
        ],
        "all_target_member_evidence": [
            {"target_member": member, "source_member_fanin": source_fanin[member],
             "distinct_caller_classes": len({row["source_class"] for _, row in incoming if row["target_member"] == member}),
             "csv_row_numbers_including_header": [number for number, row in incoming if row["target_member"] == member],
             "example_rows": [evidence_row(number, row) for number, row in incoming if row["target_member"] == member][:2]}
            for member in sorted(targets)
        ],
        "strongest_caller_member_rows": [evidence_row(number, row) for number, row in incoming if row["source_class"] == strongest],
    }


def resolution_audit(graph):
    rows = read_csv(graph / "unresolved_member_uses.csv")
    aliases = {row["binary_class"]: row["class"] for row in read_csv(graph / "class_aliases.csv")}
    counts = {row["metric"]: int(row["value"]) for row in read_csv(graph / "extraction_counts.csv")}
    interclass = [row for row in rows if aliases[row["source_class"]] != aliases[row["symbolic_target"]]]
    return {
        "unresolved_event_rows": len(rows),
        "distinct_recorded_references": len({tuple(row.items()) for row in rows}),
        "unresolved_interclass_event_rows_after_merging": len(interclass),
        "distinct_unresolved_interclass_references_after_merging": len({tuple(row.items()) for row in interclass}),
        "observed_member_use_events": counts["observed_member_use_events"],
        "unresolved_fraction_of_all_observed_member_use_events": len(rows) / counts["observed_member_use_events"],
        "fraction_interpretation": "share of raw observed events, not a completeness or false-negative rate",
        "top_unresolved_members": [{"member": key, "event_rows": count} for key, count in Counter(row["member"] for row in rows).most_common(15)],
        "top_unresolved_normalized_targets": [
            {"class": key, "event_rows": count}
            for key, count in Counter(aliases[row["symbolic_target"]] for row in rows).most_common(15)
        ],
        "interface_resolution_ties": counts.get("interface_resolution_ties", 0),
        "member_resolution_cache_entries": counts.get("member_resolution_cache_entries", 0),
        "member_resolution_cache_hits": counts.get("member_resolution_cache_hits", 0),
        "note": "Unavailable library ancestors and their inherited members are not invented as project member evidence. Raw unresolved rows can contain repeated call sites and intraclass uses.",
    }


def source_interpretation_checks(sources_jar):
    """Verify the source statements used to interpret the two numerical cases."""
    entries = {
        "com/google/common/base/Preconditions.java": [
            "public static void checkArgument(boolean expression)",
            "public static void checkState(boolean expression)",
        ],
        "com/google/common/collect/Iterables.java": [
            "return Iterators.all(iterable.iterator(), predicate);",
            "return Iterators.any(iterable.iterator(), predicate);",
            "return Iterators.find(iterable.iterator(), predicate);",
        ],
    }
    checks = []
    with ZipFile(sources_jar) as archive:
        for entry, markers in entries.items():
            data = archive.read(entry)
            lines = data.decode("utf-8").splitlines()
            found = []
            for marker in markers:
                matches = [(number, line.strip()) for number, line in enumerate(lines, start=1) if marker in line]
                assert len(matches) == 1, (entry, marker, matches)
                found.append({"line_number": matches[0][0], "source_line": matches[0][1]})
            checks.append({"archive_entry": entry, "entry_sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "verified_markers": found})
    return checks

Both thresholds pass, so UE removes Preconditions.", "",
        "Even its strongest caller has a small share of the class's dedication. Its argument and state validation functions are broadly shared in this member-use graph. def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--version", default="33.7.2")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    graph = ROOT / "data/graphs" / args.version
    source_jars = sorted((ROOT / "data/raw" / args.version).glob("*sources.jar"))
    assert len(source_jars) == 1
    sources_jar = source_jars[0]
    result_path = ROOT / "results/versions" / args.version / "ue.json"
    production_json = json.loads(result_path.read_text())
    production = {row["class_name"]: row for row in production_json["scores"]}
    thresholds = (Fraction(str(production_json["usage_threshold"])), Fraction(str(production_json["cardinality_threshold"])))
    assert thresholds == (Fraction(17, 20), Fraction(9, 10))
    classes = {row["class"] for row in read_csv(graph / "classes.csv")}
    rows = list(enumerate(read_csv(graph / "member_dependencies.csv"), start=2))
    cases = [exact_case(name, rows, classes, production, thresholds) for name in (
        "com.google.common.base.Preconditions", "com.google.common.collect.Iterators",
    )]
    assert cases[0]["exact"]["omnipresent"] and not cases[1]["exact"]["omnipresent"]
    # Guard the prose's concrete interpretation if a different release is used.
    assert cases[1]["exact"]["strongest_caller"] == "com.google.common.collect.Iterables"
    for case in cases:
        assert case["incoming_member_row_count"] == case["distinct_incoming_member_pairs"]
    assert {
        "com.google.common.collect.ImmutableList",
        "com.google.common.collect.ImmutableMap",
        "com.google.common.collect.ImmutableCollection",
    }.issubset(production_json["removed_classes"])
    inputs = [graph / name for name in ("classes.csv", "member_dependencies.csv", "class_aliases.csv", "unresolved_member_uses.csv", "extraction_counts.csv", "provenance.json")]
    inputs += [result_path, Path(__file__).resolve(), sources_jar]
    release_provenance = json.loads((graph / "provenance.json").read_text())
    assert hashlib.sha256(sources_jar.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == release_provenance["sources_sha256"]
    audit = {
        "schema_version": 1, "version": args.version,
        "checked_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "method": "independent per-class set definitions and exact Fraction arithmetic; production UE is not imported",
        "source_row_numbering": "CSV row number including the header as row 1",
        "input_sha256": {str(path.relative_to(ROOT)): hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for path in inputs},
        "thresholds": {"usage_equality": rational(thresholds[0]), "cardinality": rational(thresholds[1])},
        "release_provenance": release_provenance,
        "source_interpretation_checks": source_interpretation_checks(sources_jar),
        "removed_classes_total": len(production_json["removed_classes"]),
        "removed_classes": production_json["removed_classes"],
        "cases": cases, "resolution_coverage": resolution_audit(graph),
        "all_checks_passed": True,
    }
    output = ROOT / "results/performance/case_checks.json"
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(audit, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
    write_report(ROOT / "docs/ue_case_study.md", audit)
    for case in cases:
        print(case["class_name"], "exact maximum =", case["exact"]["max_dedication"]["fraction"], "decision verified")
    print(f"Saved {output} and docs/ue_case_study.md")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independently audit the 30-release Guava study using only Python's stdlib.

No production extraction, detector, clustering or metric function is imported.
This is an artifact audit, not another architecture experiment. It recomputes
the lecture formulas and exact-rational UE decisions from stored graph evidence,
checks archive membership/provenance, and checks all 120 partition populations.
Run after scripts/run_study.py: python scripts/validate_results.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter, defaultdict, deque
import csv
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from fractions import Fraction
import hashlib
from itertools import combinations
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys
import zipfile


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RELATIONS = {"EXTENDS", "IMPLEMENTS", "METHOD_CALL", "METHOD_PARAMETER",
             "FIELD_REFERENCE", "TYPE_USAGE", "EXCEPTION_THROWS"}
OWNED_PREFIXES = ("com.google.common.", "com.google.thirdparty.publicsuffix.")
SOURCE_PREFIXES = ("com/google/common/", "com/google/thirdparty/publicsuffix/")
INSTRUCTOR_SHA256 = "8e4e857222dba569cc48ddf2bc58d3ac206d4ee1c0d5228b7b69517016450e9c"


def rows(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def data(path: Path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        return hashlib.file_digest(handle, "sha256").hexdigest()


def close(a, b) -> bool:
    a = None if a == "" else a
    b = None if b == "" else b
    if a is None or b is None:
        return a is b
    try:
        return math.isfinite(float(a)) and math.isfinite(float(b)) and math.isclose(
            float(a), float(b), rel_tol=1e-11, abs_tol=1e-12)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


def independent_quality(nodes, edges, assignment) -> dict:
    """Lecture formula by edge contributions, independent of saved term tables."""
    sizes = Counter(assignment.values())
    k, n, m = len(sizes), len(nodes), len(edges)
    pair_count = k * (k - 1) // 2
    inner, incoming, outgoing, cross = Counter(), Counter(), Counter(), Counter()
    cohesion_terms, coupling_terms = [], []
    for a, b in edges:
        left, right = assignment[a], assignment[b]
        if left == right:
            inner[left] += 1
            cohesion_terms.append(1 / (k * sizes[left] * (sizes[left] - 1)))
        else:
            incoming[right] += 1
            outgoing[left] += 1
            cross[tuple(sorted((left, right)))] += 1
            coupling_terms.append(1 / (pair_count * 2 * sizes[left] * sizes[right]))
    cohesion, coupling = math.fsum(cohesion_terms), math.fsum(coupling_terms)
    internal = sum(inner.values())
    capacity = sum(s * (s - 1) for s in sizes.values())
    cluster_terms = [{
        "cluster": c, "n_classes": s, "internal_edges": inner[c],
        "possible_internal_edges": s * (s - 1),
        "cohesion": inner[c] / (s * (s - 1)) if s > 1 else 0.0,
        "incoming_inter_edges": incoming[c], "outgoing_inter_edges": outgoing[c],
    } for c, s in sorted(sizes.items())]
    pair_terms = [{
        "cluster_i": a, "cluster_j": b, "n_i": sizes[a], "n_j": sizes[b],
        "inter_edges_both_directions": cross[a, b],
        "possible_inter_edges": 2 * sizes[a] * sizes[b],
        "coupling": cross[a, b] / (2 * sizes[a] * sizes[b]),
    } for a, b in combinations(sorted(sizes), 2)]
    return {
        "n_nodes": n, "n_edges": m, "n_clusters": k,
        "cohesion": cohesion, "coupling": coupling, "mq": cohesion - coupling,
        "size_weighted_cohesion": math.fsum(
            inner[c] / (s - 1) for c, s in sizes.items() if s > 1) / n if n else 0.0,
        "pair_weighted_cohesion": internal / capacity if capacity else 0.0,
        "internal_edges": internal, "inter_edges": m - internal,
        "inter_edge_fraction": (m - internal) / m if m else 0.0,
        "graph_density": m / (n * (n - 1)) if n > 1 else 0.0,
        "isolates": len(set(nodes) - {x for edge in edges for x in edge}),
        "min_cluster_size": min(sizes.values(), default=0),
        "max_cluster_size": max(sizes.values(), default=0),
        "mean_cluster_size": n / k if k else 0.0,
        "singleton_clusters": sum(s == 1 for s in sizes.values()),
        "per_cluster": cluster_terms, "per_pair": pair_terms,
    }


def independent_matching(options, right_count):
    """Unit-capacity max flow, separate from production's matching implementation."""
    left_count = len(options)
    sink = left_count + right_count + 1
    capacity = [dict() for _ in range(sink + 1)]

    def connect(a, b):
        capacity[a][b], capacity[b][a] = 1, 0

    for i, targets in enumerate(options, 1):
        connect(0, i)
        for j in targets:
            connect(i, 1 + left_count + j)
    for j in range(right_count):
        connect(1 + left_count + j, sink)
    flow = 0
    while True:
        predecessors = {0: -1}
        queue = deque([0])
        while queue and sink not in predecessors:
            vertex = queue.popleft()
            for neighbor, available in capacity[vertex].items():
                if available and neighbor not in predecessors:
                    predecessors[neighbor] = vertex
                    queue.append(neighbor)
        if sink not in predecessors:
            return flow
        vertex = sink
        while vertex:
            predecessor = predecessors[vertex]
            capacity[predecessor][vertex] -= 1
            capacity[vertex][predecessor] += 1
            vertex = predecessor
        flow += 1


def independent_mojofm(a, b, nodes) -> dict:
    """Recompute both MoJo terms with flow; use a different denominator algorithm.

    A maximum flow on the tied intersection maxima gives occupied target groups.
    For the denominator, build the target-size Ferrers graph directly: a target
    cluster of size s connects to the first s artificial groups. Its maximum
    matching gives the worst-case group count. This does not reuse production's
    sorted-size recurrence. No production module is used in this check.
    """
    nodes = tuple(nodes)
    left = {label: i for i, label in enumerate(sorted({a[n] for n in nodes}))}
    right = {label: i for i, label in enumerate(sorted({b[n] for n in nodes}))}
    matrix = [[0] * len(right) for _ in left]
    for node in nodes:
        matrix[left[a[node]]][right[b[node]]] += 1
    maxima = [max(row) for row in matrix]
    options = [[j for j, value in enumerate(row) if value == maximum]
               for row, maximum in zip(matrix, maxima)]
    groups = independent_matching(options, len(right))
    moves, joins = len(nodes) - sum(maxima), len(left) - groups
    target_sizes = list(Counter(b[n] for n in nodes).values())
    normalizer_groups = independent_matching([range(size) for size in target_sizes],
                                             max(target_sizes, default=0))
    maximum = len(nodes) - normalizer_groups
    distance = moves + joins
    return {
        "direction": "source_to_target", "n_nodes": len(nodes),
        "source_clusters": len(left), "target_clusters": len(right),
        "move_operations": moves, "join_operations": joins,
        "matched_target_clusters": groups, "normalizer_groups": normalizer_groups,
        "distance": distance, "max_distance": maximum,
        "score": float(Fraction(100 * (maximum - distance), maximum)) if maximum else None,
        "defined": maximum > 0,
        "reason": None if maximum else ("empty_universe" if not nodes else "single_node_universe"),
    }


def independent_inertia(nodes, edges, assignment):
    """Compute normalized [A+I,(A+I)^T] SSE using the centroid sum identity."""
    coordinates = {n: {(0, n), (1, n)} for n in nodes}
    for source, target in edges:
        coordinates[source].add((0, target))
        coordinates[target].add((1, source))
    sizes = Counter(assignment.values())
    sums = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(float))
    for node in nodes:
        value = 1 / math.sqrt(len(coordinates[node]))
        for coordinate in sorted(coordinates[node]):
            sums[assignment[node]][coordinate] += value
    return len(nodes) - math.fsum(value * value / sizes[group]
                                 for group, vector in sums.items() for value in vector.values())


def independent_ue(nodes, member_rows, usage, cardinality) -> dict:
    """Re-evaluate paper decisions with exact fractions for every target class."""
    sources, callers, targets = defaultdict(set), defaultdict(set), defaultdict(set)
    for row in member_rows:
        sources[row["target_member"]].add(row["source_member"])
        callers[row["target_member"]].add(row["source_class"])
        targets[row["target_class"]].add(row["target_member"])
    answer = {}
    for node in nodes:
        member_set = targets[node]
        by_caller = defaultdict(Fraction)
        for target in member_set:
            contribution = Fraction(1, len(sources[target]) * len(member_set))
            for caller in callers[target]:
                by_caller[caller] += contribution
        if not by_caller:
            answer[node] = {"fanin": 0, "referenced_members": 0,
                            "max_dedication": None, "usage_equality": None,
                            "cardinality": None, "omnipresent": False}
            continue
        maximum = max(by_caller.values())
        ue = 1 - maximum
        c = 1 - Fraction(1, len(by_caller))
        answer[node] = {"fanin": len(by_caller), "referenced_members": len(member_set),
                        "max_dedication": float(maximum), "usage_equality": float(ue),
                        "cardinality": float(c),
                        "omnipresent": ue >= Fraction(str(usage)) and c >= Fraction(str(cardinality))}
    return answer


def failure_value(value):
    """Keep failures JSON-readable even for paths, bytes, sets and tuple keys."""
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(key): failure_value(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (set, frozenset)):
        return [failure_value(item) for item in sorted(value, key=str)]
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [failure_value(item) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, bytes):
        return {"bytes": len(value), "sha256": hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()}
    return value


class Audit:
    def __init__(self):
        self.counts = Counter()
        self.failures = []
        self.warnings = []
        self.release_evidence = []
        self.ai_evidence = None

    def check(self, condition, name, context, actual=None, expected=None):
        self.counts[name] += 1
        if not condition:
            self.failures.append({"check": name, "context": context,
                                  "actual": failure_value(actual), "expected": failure_value(expected)})

    def equal(self, actual, expected, name, context):
        self.check(actual == expected, name, context, actual, expected)

    def numeric(self, actual, expected, name, context):
        self.check(close(actual, expected), name, context, actual, expected)

    def flat_metrics(self, observed, expected, context):
        for key, value in expected.items():
            if key not in ("per_cluster", "per_pair"):
                self.numeric(observed.get(key), value, "independent_metric_recalculation", f"{context}/{key}")
        self.numeric(float(observed["cohesion"]) - float(observed["coupling"]), observed["mq"],
                     "mq_identity", context)
        for key in ("cohesion", "coupling", "graph_density", "inter_edge_fraction",
                    "size_weighted_cohesion", "pair_weighted_cohesion"):
            value = float(observed[key])
            self.check(0 <= value <= 1, "metric_bounds", f"{context}/{key}", value, "[0,1]")
        self.check(-1 <= float(observed["mq"]) <= 1, "metric_bounds", f"{context}/mq")

    def terms(self, observed, expected, context):
        self.equal(len(observed), len(expected), "term_table_row_counts", context)
        for index, (got, want) in enumerate(zip(observed, expected)):
            for key, value in want.items():
                name = f"{context}/{index}/{key}"
                if isinstance(value, str):
                    self.equal(got.get(key), value, "term_identity", name)
                else:
                    self.numeric(got.get(key), value, "term_value", name)


def read_edges(path: Path):
    return [(r["source"], r["target"]) for r in rows(path)]


def read_rsf(path: Path, relation: str):
    result = []
    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        tokens = line.split()
        if len(tokens) != 3 or tokens[0] != relation:
            raise ValueError(f"Malformed {relation} RSF in {path}: {line}")
        result.append((tokens[1], tokens[2]))
    return result


def read_partition(path: Path, expected_nodes, audit: Audit):
    records = rows(path)
    assignment = {r["class"]: r["cluster"] for r in records}
    audit.equal(len(records), len(assignment), "unique_membership", str(path))
    audit.equal(set(assignment), set(expected_nodes), "complete_membership", str(path))
    audit.check(all(assignment.values()), "nonempty_cluster_labels", str(path))
    rsf = read_rsf(path.with_suffix(".rsf"), "contain")
    audit.equal(sorted(rsf), sorted((c, n) for n, c in assignment.items()), "partition_rsf_matches_csv", str(path))
    return assignment


def independent_calendar_selection(groups, count):
    """Reconstruct calendar selection by explicitly comparing squared-gap sums.

    The selector scores an insertion by its local product formula. This audit
    instead inserts every eligible candidate and subtracts the complete sums of
    squared calendar gaps. Only frozen release metadata enters either method.
    """
    key = lambda version: tuple(map(int, (version + ".0.0").split(".")[:3]))
    day = lambda row: date.fromisoformat(row["release_date"][:10]).toordinal()
    order = lambda row: (day(row), key(row["version"]))
    family = lambda row: ".".join(row["version"].split(".")[:2])
    representatives = {name: min(candidates, key=order) for name, candidates in groups.items()}
    endpoint = max((row for candidates in groups.values() for row in candidates), key=order)
    representatives[family(endpoint)] = endpoint
    anchors = [representatives[f"{major}.0"] for major in range(10, 34)]
    anchors.append(endpoint)
    selected = {family(row): row for row in anchors}
    optional_per_major = Counter()
    steps = []

    def gaps(ordered):
        return [day(b) - day(a) for a, b in zip(ordered, ordered[1:])]

    while len(selected) < count:
        current = sorted(selected.values(), key=order)
        before = sum(gap * gap for gap in gaps(current))
        candidates = []
        for name, row in representatives.items():
            major = key(row["version"])[0]
            if name in selected or optional_per_major[major] >= 2:
                continue
            with_candidate = sorted([*current, row], key=order)
            current_gaps = gaps(with_candidate)
            if min(current_gaps) < 60:
                continue
            position = with_candidate.index(row)
            if position == 0 or position == len(with_candidate) - 1:
                continue
            gain = before - sum(gap * gap for gap in current_gaps)
            candidates.append((gain, row, with_candidate[position - 1], with_candidate[position + 1]))
        if not candidates:
            raise ValueError("Frozen calendar-selection constraints cannot supply 30 releases")
        gain, chosen, left, right = min(candidates, key=lambda item: (-item[0], *order(item[1])))
        selected[family(chosen)] = chosen
